# 현행 작업: v2 6단계 — 격리 범위 추적과 로트 ID

상태: **사용자 확정** (2026-10-31). 결정 근거는 `handoff/decisions.md`. 1–5단계(아래 명세)는 완료이며, 그 동작과 테스트는 보존한다.

## 목표와 경계
- 설비가 멈췄을 때 **이미 PASS로 지나간 제품 중 어디까지 의심할지**를 뽑고, 방식별 포착률과 격리량을 비교한다.
- 격리는 **분석 보고서**다. 제품 상태, 보류, 재검사를 바꾸지 않는다.
- 분포·확률은 모두 가상 가정이다. 실제 설비 진단이나 SL 기준이 아니다. 기존 고정 문장을 유지한다.
- 제외: 측정값, 관리도, 실제 시각, 생산 중간 고장, 자동 격리, 외부 패키지. 측정값 관련 내용은 7단계에서 따로 명세한다.

## 왜 생성 가정을 바꾸는가
- 기존 결함품은 설비와 무관하게 FAIL 90%, PASS 10%다. 고장 때문에 **더 놓치는** 효과는 모델링되어 있지 않다.
- 현행 설정(고장 FAIL 30%, 결함 2%, 추가 미검출 50%)은 간이 계산에서 실행당 유출이 약 0.11건으로 비교가 성립하지 않는다.
- 그래서 **미세 고장**으로 바꾼다. 아래 값의 간이 계산은 첫 정지까지 약 46검사, 실행당 유출 약 1.26건, 유출 있는 실행 약 56%다. 이 수치는 고장 설비 1대만 본 독립 추정이며 실제 시드 결과가 아니다.

## 보존 조건
- `lot_tracking`이 없거나 `false`이면 v1·1–5단계의 입력·출력·종료 코드·난수 흐름은 바이트까지 같다.
- `src/rules.py`와 기존 테스트 8개 파일(`test_rules`, `test_recipe`, `test_retest`, `test_equipment`, `test_generate`, `test_summary`, `test_restart`, `test_evaluate`)은 수정하지 않는다. 새 테스트는 `tests/test_containment.py`에 둔다.
- 기존 설정 파일, 샘플, `eval_dev`·`eval_holdout`·`eval_baseline` 보고서는 바꾸지 않는다.
- 생성기 버전은 `lot_size`가 있는 설정에서만 `1.3.0`이다. 그 밖의 설정은 기존 버전을 유지한다.

## 모드와 헤더
| 설비 파일 `lot_tracking` | CSV 헤더 | products 열 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 없음 또는 `false` | 기존 7열. 8열이면 종료 코드 2 | 기존과 같음 |
| `true` | `seq,product_id,event,inspection,result,model,equipment,lot`. 7열이면 종료 코드 2 | `model` 뒤에 `lot` |

- bool 외의 값은 종료 코드 3이다.
- `--equipment` 없이는 로트 모드를 쓸 수 없다. `restart_enabled`와는 독립이다.

## 입력 규칙
`REGISTER`에서만 `lot`을 쓴다. 형식은 `^[A-Z0-9_-]+$`이며 대소문자를 정리하지 않고 비교한다.

검사 순서: 열 구조 → `seq` → `lot` → 그 뒤는 기존 순서. 재가동 행도 같은 순서를 쓰므로, `lot`이 잘못되면 다른 오류가 함께 있어도 `lot` 사유로 거절한다.

| 입력 | 판정 | 이유 문구 | 사유 코드 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `REGISTER`의 lot 빈칸·형식 오류 | `RULE_PRESERVE` 거절 | `lot이 비어 있거나 형식이 잘못됨` | `PRESERVE_BAD_LOT` |
| `REGISTER` 외 행(재가동 포함)의 lot 기입 | `RULE_PRESERVE` 거절 | `REGISTER 이외의 lot은 빈칸이어야 함` | `PRESERVE_LOT_FIELD` |

이미 등록된 제품을 다른 lot으로 다시 등록하면 기존처럼 `이미 등록된 제품`으로 거절하며 lot은 바뀌지 않는다.

## 격리 보고서 (`--containment`)
`--equipment`가 있을 때만 쓴다. 없으면 종료 코드 2다. 전체 입력 처리 뒤 `reports/<입력>_containment.csv`를 쓴다.

**대상**: 정지 S의 설비 E에서, S 행 이전(S 행 포함)에 `(제품, 검사)` 기준 허용된 **최초 검사 PASS**를 받은 제품이다.
- 재검사 PASS는 대상이 아니다.
- 다른 검사로 보류·폐기된 제품도 포함한다. 불량이 물리적으로 지나갔는지를 보기 때문이다.
- 모든 방식은 **정지 시점까지의 행만** 본다.

**구간**: E에 대해 **허용된** 가장 최근 `EQUIP_RESTART` 다음 행부터 S까지다. 없으면 입력 시작부터다. 다른 설비의 재가동과 거절된 재가동은 구간을 바꾸지 않는다. 모든 방식은 이 구간을 넘어 거슬러 가지 않는다.

| 방식 | 의심 범위 |
| --- | --- |
| `window` | 정지 창 안의 PASS 제품 |
| `last_10` | 구간 안, S를 포함한 E의 허용 최초 검사 최근 10건 중 PASS 제품 |
| `last_20` | 같은 조건의 최근 20건 중 PASS 제품 |
| `lot` | 구간 안에서 E를 PASS했고, 정지 창의 FAIL 제품들이 속한 로트에 있는 제품. `lot_tracking`이 켜져 있을 때만 |
| `since_restart` | 구간 안에서 E를 PASS한 제품 전체 |

`since_restart`는 그 구간의 유출을 구조적으로 모두 포함한다. 기준선(상한)으로 해석한다.

열: `stop_key,equipment,method,row_index,seq,product_id,lot,final_state`
- `row_index`, `seq`는 E의 최초 PASS 행이다. `final_state`는 입력 전체 처리 후 상태다.
- 정렬은 정지 순서 → 위 표의 방식 순서 → `row_index`다. 물리적 행 순서이므로 `seq` 중복·감소와 무관하다.
- 범위가 없는 정지는 행을 쓰지 않는다. 정지가 없으면 헤더만 쓴다.
- 터미널에는 정지·방식별 건수만 표로 낸다.

## 생성기
새 파일 `recipes/equipment_lot.json`(재가동 설비 + `lot_tracking: true`)과 설정 2개를 만든다. 값은 `generator_restart.json`을 잇고 아래만 다르다.

| 키 | `generator_lot.json` | `generator_lot_clustered.json` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `faulty_equipment` | `{"LEAK-2": 0.10}` | 같음 |
| `lot_size` | 20 | 같음 |
| `faulty_detection_loss` | `{"LEAK-2": 0.5}` | 같음 |
| `defective_product_probability` | 0.05 | 사용하지 않음 |
| `defective_lot_probability` | 없음 | 0.25 |
| `defective_in_lot_probability` | 없음 | 0.2 |

- 두 설정의 기대 결함률은 모두 5%다.
- lot ID는 등록 순서대로 `LOT-0001`부터 `lot_size`개씩 묶는다.
- `faulty_detection_loss`는 수리 전 고장 설비에서 결함품의 FAIL 확률을 `defective_fail_probability × (1 − loss)`로 낮춘다. 값 0.5면 FAIL 0.45, PASS 0.55다. 재검사에도 같은 식을 쓴다. 수리 뒤에는 기존 0.9로 돌아간다.
- **유출**: 결함품이 수리 전 고장 설비에서 최초 검사 PASS를 받은 경우의 제품이다. 결함 여부와 유출은 CSV에 넣지 않고 판정기에 주지 않는다.
- meta에 `lot_size`, `leaked_products`를 추가한다. 평가는 생성기가 기록한 유출 집합을 쓰고, meta의 값은 그 집합을 정렬해 저장한 것이다.
- `src/main.py`의 meta 검증: `leaked_products`는 비어 있지 않은 문자열 배열 항목만 갖고, 중복이 없고, `defective_products`의 부분집합이어야 한다. `defective_products` 없이 `leaked_products`만 있으면 오류다. 등록 제품 여부는 summary에서 기존 `defective_products`와 같이 확인한다. 위반은 종료 코드 2다.
- 오류 주입 5종은 그대로다. 주입 행의 lot은 빈칸이고, 재가동 행은 기존처럼 후보에서 뺀다.

설정 검증(위반은 생성·평가 모두 종료 코드 2, 파일 미생성):
- `lot_size`는 bool을 제외한 양의 정수다. 마지막 로트는 `lot_size`보다 작을 수 있다.
- `faulty_detection_loss`, `defective_lot_probability`, `defective_in_lot_probability`는 유한한 0–1 값이다.
- `faulty_detection_loss`의 키는 `faulty_equipment`에 있는 설비여야 한다. 없는 설비의 손실은 0이다.
- `lot_size` 없이 `faulty_detection_loss`나 집중 로트 키를 쓰면 오류다. 집중 로트 키는 둘 다 있거나 둘 다 없어야 한다.
- `lot_size`가 있으면 설비 파일의 `lot_tracking`이 `true`여야 한다.

## 평가 (`py -m src.evaluate --containment`)

```powershell
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_dev
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot_clustered.json --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_clustered_dev
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 1000-1099 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_holdout --holdout --selected-from reports/eval_lot_dev_containment.csv
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot_clustered.json --seeds 1000-1099 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_clustered_holdout --holdout --selected-from reports/eval_lot_clustered_dev_containment.csv
```

- 정지 규칙은 3:2로 고정한다. 이 단계는 정지 규칙이 아니라 격리 방식을 비교한다. `--containment`에서는 규칙 목록이 정확히 `3:2` 하나여야 한다.
- 설정에 `lot_size`가 없으면 종료 코드 2다. 고장 설비가 여러 대면 기존처럼 거절한다.
- `--containment --holdout`은 `--selected-from`이 필수다. 개발 보고서의 선택 행과 설정 SHA-256을 읽는다. 파일이 없거나, 선택 행이 없거나, SHA가 다르면 종료 코드 2다.
- 개발에서 선택이 없었으면(표본 부족 또는 기준 충족 방식 없음) 홀드아웃은 `검증할 선택 없음`으로 표시하고 종료 코드 0이다.
- 홀드아웃 보고서는 모든 방식의 지표와 **선택 방식의 최종 판정**을 구분해 쓴다. 판정은 선택 방식에 대해서만 낸다.
- 출력은 `reports/<out>_containment.csv`다. 고정 문장, 설정 SHA-256, 생성기 버전을 함께 기록한다.

### 지표 (전체 합산)
실행 r의 유출 집합을 `E_r`, 방식 m이 그 실행의 모든 정지에서 고른 **고유 제품 합집합**을 `C_m,r`로 둔다. 실행별 비율의 평균은 쓰지 않는다.

| 지표 | 식 |
| --- | --- |
| 포착률 | `Σ|C ∩ E| ÷ Σ|E|` |
| 평균 격리량 | `Σ|C| ÷ 실행 수` |
| 격리 정밀도 | `Σ|C ∩ E| ÷ Σ|C|` |
| 평균 잔류 유출 | `Σ|E − C| ÷ 실행 수` |

- 분모가 0이면 빈칸이다. 함께 전체 실행 수, 유출 있는 실행 수, 유출품 합계, 정지 합계를 표시한다.
- 비율은 소수 넷째 자리, 평균은 소수 둘째 자리 `ROUND_HALF_UP`이다.

### 사전 선택 기준 (결과를 보기 전에 고정)
1. **표본 조건**: 개발 시드에서 유출품 합계 ≥ 30이고 유출 있는 실행 ≥ 20이어야 한다. 미달이면 `평가 표본 부족`으로 끝내고 선택하지 않는다.
2. 포착률 ≥ **0.90**인 방식만 남긴다. 정확히 0.90은 통과다.
3. 평균 격리량이 가장 작은 방식을 고른다. 동률이면 위 표의 방식 순서를 쓴다.
4. 독립·집중 설정을 **각각** 판정한다. 후보가 없으면 `기준 충족 방식 없음`으로 끝내고 기준을 완화하지 않는다.
5. 홀드아웃 시드 1000–1099는 개발에서 선택한 방식이 1·2번 조건을 유지하는지만 본다. 표본 조건 미달도 `미확인`이다. 실패해도 다시 고르지 않는다.

## 사전 기대표 (사용자 확정, 2026-10-31. 구현 결과에 맞춰 바꾸지 않는다)
T68–T74 공통: `lamp_models_retest.json`, 재가동·로트 모드, 3:2 규칙. 제품은 B 모델로 등록하고 P1~P2를 마친 상태다. T70·T71은 제품마다 lot을 달리 쓰지 않아도 되는 사례다.

| ID | 입력 | 기대 결과 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| T67 | 신규 제품 z를 B 모델, lot `L1`로 등록 | 허용. products의 `lot`은 L1 |
| T68 | ① lot 빈칸 등록 ② `l-1` 등록 ③ P1 행에 lot 기입 ④ 재가동 행에 lot 기입 ⑤ 재가동 행에 lot 기입 + `result=OK` ⑥ 등록된 제품을 다른 lot으로 재등록 | ①② `PRESERVE_BAD_LOT` ③④⑤ `PRESERVE_LOT_FIELD` ⑥ `이미 등록된 제품`. 모두 제품·설비 상태와 lot 불변 |
| T69 | ① 키 없는 설비 파일 + 7열 ② `lot_tracking: false` + 7열 ③ 키 없음 + 8열 ④ `true` + 7열 ⑤ `"lot_tracking": 1` | ①② 기존 출력과 바이트 동일 ③④ 종료 코드 2 ⑤ 종료 코드 3 |

## 구현 순서
1. **6a**: 입력, 로트 모드, 격리 범위, 보고서, T67–T76, T86.
2. **6b**: 생성기, 평가, T77–T85, T87, T88, 개발·홀드아웃 실행.

두 묶음을 각각 커밋하고, 6b 뒤 자동 검토 루프를 한 번 돌린다. 최초 검수는 `gpt-6-astra`, 재검수는 `gpt-6.1-sol`이다.

## 구현 시 주의점
- `EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS`는 생성기·판정기·`_invalid_fields`가 함께 쓴다. 로트 열은 모드로 분기하고, 기존 상수를 바꾸지 않는다.
- `_invalid_fields`의 재가동 분기는 일반 검사보다 앞에 있다. lot 검사를 그보다 먼저 둔다.
- `Generator.product`의 결함 추출과 집중 로트 추출은 같은 난수 흐름을 쓴다. `lot_size`가 없으면 난수 소비가 바뀌지 않아야 한다.
- `summary.py`의 `fail_causes`, `stop_history`를 재사용한다. 정지 전 창은 `EquipmentStop.window`에 있다.

- `src/containment.py`, `src/equipment.py`, `src/recipe_rules.py`, `src/reason_codes.py`, `src/recipe_reports.py`, `src/main.py`, `src/generate.py`, `src/evaluate.py`
- `recipes/equipment_lot.json`, `recipes/generator_lot.json`, `recipes/generator_lot_clustered.json`, 샘플 `data/sample_events_lot.csv`와 보고서, `reports/eval_lot_*`
- `tests/test_containment.py`, README 「격리 범위와 로트 (v2 6단계)」 절

# v2 5단계 명세 (완료, 2026-10-31 · 리뷰 011 PASS)

상태: **사용자 확정** (2026-10-30). 결정 근거는 이 명세의 「사용자 결정」 표. 1–4단계(아래 명세)는 완료이며, 그 동작과 테스트는 보존한다.

## 목표와 경계
- 정지만 있고 복구가 없는 3단계 설비 모델에 **점검 후 재가동**을 추가한다.
- "규칙대로 판정하는가"를 넘어 **"이 정지 규칙이 좋은 규칙인가"**를 여러 시드의 수치로 평가한다.
- 정지 규칙 선택 기준은 **결과를 보기 전에 고정**하고, 선택에 쓰지 않은 시드로 다시 확인한다.
- 생성 확률·점검 소요·수리 효과는 모두 심어 놓은 가정이다. 결과는 실제 설비 진단이나 공정 개선의 증거가 아니다. 기존 고정 문장을 유지한다.
- 제외: 실제 시각·UPH·OEE, 공정 설비, 로트 ID, 측정값·Cpk, 그래프, 외부 패키지, 고장이 생산 중간에 시작되는 모델, 재가동 취소.

@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+"""설비 정지 시점까지 그 설비를 PASS한 제품 중 어디까지 의심할지 뽑는 분석. 제품 상태는 바꾸지 않는다."""
+
+from collections import Counter
+import csv
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+METHODS = ("window", "last_10", "last_20", "lot", "since_restart")
+CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS = ("stop_key", "equipment", "method", "row_index", "seq", "product_id", "lot", "final_state")
+
+
+def _passes(records):
+    return [record for record in records if record.result == "PASS"]
+
+
+def stop_scopes(analysis):
+    """정지 순서대로 (stop_key, 정지, 방식, 최초 PASS 기록들)을 돌려준다."""
+    products = analysis.process.products
+    lot_tracking = analysis.process.equipment_book.lot_tracking
+    numbers = Counter()
+    scopes = []
+    for stop in analysis.stop_history:
+        numbers[stop.equipment] += 1
+        # 허용된 같은 설비의 재가동만 구간을 나눈다.
+        start = max((item.row_index for item in analysis.restarts
+                     if item.equipment == stop.equipment and item.row_index < stop.row_index), default=0)
+        records = [record for record in analysis.first_inspections
+                   if record.equipment == stop.equipment and start < record.row_index <= stop.row_index]
+        chosen = {
+            "window": _passes(stop.window),
+            "last_10": _passes(records[-10:]),
+            "last_20": _passes(records[-20:]),
+            "since_restart": _passes(records),
+        }
+        if lot_tracking:
+            lots = {products[item.product_id].lot for item in stop.fail_causes}
+            chosen["lot"] = [record for record in _passes(records) if products[record.product_id].lot in lots]
+        key = stop.equipment + "#" + str(numbers[stop.equipment])
+        scopes.extend((key, stop, method, chosen[method]) for method in METHODS if method in chosen)
+    return scopes
+
+
+def build_containment(analysis):
+    products = analysis.process.products
+    return [dict(zip(CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS, (
+        key, stop.equipment, method, record.row_index, record.seq, record.product_id,
+        products[record.product_id].lot, products[record.product_id].status,
+    ))) for key, stop, method, records in stop_scopes(analysis) for record in records]
+
+
+def run_scopes(analysis):
+    """방식별로 한 실행의 모든 정지에서 고른 고유 제품 합집합."""
+    methods = {}
+    for _, _, method, records in stop_scopes(analysis):
+        methods.setdefault(method, set()).update(record.product_id for record in records)
+    return methods
+
+
+def write_containment(stem, rows):
+    path = Path("reports") / (str(stem) + "_containment.csv")
+    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS)
+        writer.writeheader()
+        writer.writerows(rows)
+    return path
+
+
+def print_containment(rows, path):
+    print("[격리 범위]")
+    print("stop_key | method | product_count")
+    for (key, method), count in Counter((row["stop_key"], row["method"]) for row in rows).items():
+        print(key + " | " + method + " | " + str(count))
+    print()
+    print("저장 파일: " + path.as_posix())

+def containment_metrics(runs):
+    """runs는 (유출 집합, {방식: 고유 제품 합집합}) 목록이다. 실행별 비율은 평균하지 않고 전체를 합산한다."""
+    count = len(runs)
+    leaks = sum(len(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    methods = {}
+    for method in METHODS:
+        if not any(method in scopes for _, scopes in runs):
+            continue
+        chosen = [(leaked, scopes.get(method, set())) for leaked, scopes in runs]
+        caught = sum(len(leaked & scope) for leaked, scope in chosen)
+        total = sum(len(scope) for _, scope in chosen)
+        methods[method] = {
+            "capture_rate": Fraction(caught, leaks) if leaks else None,
+            "mean_quarantine": Fraction(total, count),
+            "precision": Fraction(caught, total) if total else None,
+            "mean_residual": Fraction(leaks - caught, count),
+        }
+    return methods
+
+
+def sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs):
+    return leaks >= MIN_LEAKS and leak_runs >= MIN_LEAK_RUNS
+
+
+def capture_met(metrics):
+    return metrics["capture_rate"] is not None and metrics["capture_rate"] >= MIN_CAPTURE
+
+
+def select_method(method_metrics, leaks, leak_runs):
+    """(선택 방식, 표시 문구). 후보가 없으면 완화하지 않는다."""
+    if not sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs):
+        return None, LOW_SAMPLE
+    candidates = [method for method, metrics in method_metrics.items() if capture_met(metrics)]
+    if not candidates:
+        return None, NO_METHOD
+    return min(candidates, key=lambda method: (method_metrics[method]["mean_quarantine"], METHODS.index(method))), "선택"
+
+
+def containment_verdict(metrics, leaks, leak_runs):
+    return "확인" if sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs) and capture_met(metrics) else "미확인"
+
+
+def read_selection(path, config_sha):
+    """개발 보고서의 선택을 읽는다. 설정이 다르면 그 선택을 확인할 수 없다."""
+    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        rows = list(csv.DictReader(source))
+    sha = next((row["value"] for row in rows if row["section"] == "provenance" and row["method"] == "config_sha256"), None)
+    selection = next((row for row in rows if row["section"] == "selection"), None)
+    if selection is None:
+        raise ValueError("개발 보고서에 선택 행이 없음: " + str(path))
+    if sha != config_sha:
+        raise ValueError("개발 보고서와 설정의 SHA-256이 다름: " + str(path))
+    if selection["method"] and selection["method"] not in METHODS:
+        raise ValueError("알 수 없는 선택 방식: " + selection["method"])
+    return selection["method"] or None
+
+
+def evaluate_containment(config_path, seeds, rules, products):
+    config, content, recipe, book = load_setup(config_path)
+    if rules != [(3, 2)]:
+        raise ValueError("--containment의 규칙은 정확히 3:2여야 함")
+    if "lot_size" not in config:
+        raise ValueError("--containment에는 lot_size 설정이 필요함")
+    faulty = set(config["faulty_equipment"])
+    if len(faulty) > 1:
+        raise ValueError("고장 설비가 여러 대인 검출률 단위는 명세에서 정해지지 않음: " + ", ".join(sorted(faulty)))
+    ruled = replace(book, window=3, fails=2)
+    runs, stops = [], 0
+    for seed in seeds:
+        generator, _, rows, _ = build_rows(seed, products, config, recipe.models, ruled)
+        analysis = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, ruled)
+        stops += len(analysis.stop_history)
+        runs.append((set(generator.leaked_products), run_scopes(analysis)))
+    provenance = {
+        "config_sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), "recipe_sha256": recipe.sha256,
+        "equipment_sha256": book.sha256, "generator_version": generator_version(config),
+        "seeds": str(seeds[0]) + "-" + str(seeds[-1]), "products": str(products),
+    }
+    return runs, stops, provenance
+
+
+def containment_rows(runs, stops, provenance, holdout, selected):
+    leaks = sum(len(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    leak_runs = sum(bool(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    metrics = containment_metrics(runs)
+    blank = dict.fromkeys(CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS, "")
+    rows = [{**blank, "section": "notice", "value": ASSUMPTION_NOTICE}]
+    rows.extend({**blank, "section": "provenance", "method": key, "value": value} for key, value in provenance.items())
+    for method, item in metrics.items():
+        rows.append({
+            **blank, "section": "method", "method": method, "runs": len(runs), "leak_runs": leak_runs, "leaks": leaks,
+            "stops": stops, "capture_rate": _decimal(item["capture_rate"], 4),
+            "mean_quarantine": _decimal(item["mean_quarantine"], 2), "precision": _decimal(item["precision"], 4),
+            "mean_residual": _decimal(item["mean_residual"], 2),
+        })
+    if holdout:
+        verdict = containment_verdict(metrics[selected], leaks, leak_runs) if selected else NO_SELECTION
+        rows.append({**blank, "section": "holdout_verdict", "method": selected or "", "value": verdict})
+    else:
+        method, text = select_method(metrics, leaks, leak_runs)
+        rows.append({**blank, "section": "selection", "method": method or "", "value": text})
+    return rows
+
+
+def print_containment_rows(rows):
+    columns = CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS[1:-1]
+    print(" | ".join(columns))
+    for row in rows:
+        if row["section"] == "method":
+            print(" | ".join(str(row[column]) for column in columns))
+    for row in rows:
+        if row["section"] in ("notice", "selection", "holdout_verdict"):
+            print((row["method"] + " " if row["method"] else "") + row["value"])
+
+

+def check_lot_config(config, book):
+    def unit(value):
+        return type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value) and 0 <= value <= 1
+
+    lot_keys = {"defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"} & config.keys()
+    if "lot_size" not in config:
+        if book.lot_tracking:
+            raise ValueError("lot_tracking 설비 파일에는 lot_size 설정이 필요함")
+        if lot_keys or "faulty_detection_loss" in config:
+            raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss와 결함 로트 설정에는 lot_size가 필요함")
+        return
+    if type(config["lot_size"]) is not int or config["lot_size"] < 1:
+        raise ValueError("lot_size는 양의 정수여야 함 (bool 불가)")
+    if not book.lot_tracking:
+        raise ValueError("lot_size 설정에는 lot_tracking 설비 파일이 필요함")
+    if lot_keys and len(lot_keys) != 2:
+        raise ValueError("defective_lot_probability와 defective_in_lot_probability는 함께 있어야 함")
+    if not all(unit(config[key]) for key in lot_keys):
+        raise ValueError("결함 로트 확률은 0–1 값이어야 함")
+    loss = config.get("faulty_detection_loss", {})
+    if not isinstance(loss, dict) or not all(unit(value) for value in loss.values()):
+        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss는 설비별 0–1 값이어야 함")
+    if not set(loss) <= set(config["faulty_equipment"]):
+        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss의 설비는 faulty_equipment에 있어야 함")
+
+
+def generator_version(config):
+    if "lot_size" in config:
+        return LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION
+    return RESTART_GENERATOR_VERSION if "restart" in config else GENERATOR_VERSION

# Intent discovery and plan requirements

Read this at Stage 2. It defines the intent summary, plan discovery, what context each reviewer receives, and the plan-readiness rules that Stage 6 (Synthesize and present) verifies requirements against.

## Plan Requirements Completeness

When a plan is provided via `plan:<path>` or discovered from PR/branch context,
inspect the contents before checking completeness. A Product Contract alone informs product intent and does not create implementation-unit obligations. When implementation units are present, check their coverage as well, including incomplete planning. Old readiness labels do not change the review scope. Legacy plans keep the existing completeness checks.

An unaddressed requirement or implementation unit is a finding on every depth path, routed by `plan_source`: `explicit` (a `plan:` argument or a PR-body link) yields a P1 finding with `autofix_class: manual` and `owner: downstream-resolver`, which enters the actionable queue; `inferred` (auto-discovered) yields a P3 finding with `autofix_class: advisory` and `owner: human`, which stays in the report only, because an inferred match is a hint, not a contract.

When `plan_group:` names a PR group of a plan that ships as several PRs, this diff builds only that group. Completeness covers that group's units and the requirements they trace to; units in the plan's other groups are out of scope here, not unaddressed. A `plan_group:` label that starts no group heading in the plan is reported in Coverage, and completeness falls back to the whole plan.

The check also runs in reverse. A behavior rule is a decision about what users or callers observe that a product owner would otherwise have made, such as silently dropping a repeated refund request. When the diff introduces a behavior rule that nothing in the plan asks for, that unrequested behavior rule is a finding on every depth path, listed with the requirements completeness results. Route it P3 with `autofix_class: advisory` and `owner: human`, whatever the `plan_source`, and never let it change the verdict. Whether the rule is wanted is the user's call, and a correct diff is no evidence either way. Internal structure, refactors, and handling of inputs a requirement already covers are not behavior rules.

Extract requirements from these shapes, in order:

1. Unified `Product Contract` -> `### Requirements`
2. Legacy top-level `## Requirements`
3. Legacy `## Requirements Trace`

For unified plans with implementation units, also extract U-IDs from
`## Implementation Units` and compare against PR body/branch context when
available. Do not require every Product Contract R-ID to map one-to-one to a
single U-ID; verify that implemented U-IDs cite the relevant R/F/AE/KTD IDs and
that no claimed U-ID is missing from the plan.

### Stage 2: Intent discovery

Understand what the change is trying to accomplish. The source of intent depends on which Stage 1 path was taken:

**PR/URL mode:** Use the PR title, body, and linked issues from `gh pr view` metadata. Supplement with commit messages from the PR if the body is sparse.

**Branch mode:** Run `git log --oneline ${BASE}..<branch-ref>` using the resolved merge-base and resolved branch ref from Stage 1. Use `<branch-ref>` (the resolved `origin/<branch>` or fetched ref), not the raw `<branch>` argument — a remote-only branch has no matching local ref, so the raw name would fail or read a stale same-named local branch.

**Standalone (current branch):** Run:

```
echo "BRANCH:" && git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD && echo "COMMITS:" && git log --oneline ${BASE}..HEAD
```

Combined with conversation context (plan section summary, PR description), write a 2-3 line intent summary:

```
Intent: Simplify tax calculation by replacing the multi-tier rate lookup
with a flat-rate computation. Must not regress edge cases in tax-exempt handling.
```

Pass this to every reviewer in their spawn prompt. Intent shapes *how hard each reviewer looks*, not which reviewers are selected. Keep any `session-settled:` annotations (from a plan or the conversation) out of this summary. Reviewers must not see settlement annotations (Stage 2b, Plan discovery).

**When intent is ambiguous:** Infer from branch name, commits, PR title/body, diff, `plan:`, and conversation. Write the best-effort intent summary and note uncertainty in Coverage — never block on a clarifying question.

### Stage 2b: Plan discovery (requirements verification)

Locate the plan document so Stage 6 can verify requirements completeness. Check these sources in priority order — stop at the first hit:

1. **`plan:` argument.** If the caller passed a plan path, use it directly. Read the file to confirm it exists.
2. **PR body.** If PR metadata was fetched in Stage 1, scan the body for paths matching `<root>/plans/*.{md,html}` (unified plans may be markdown or HTML). If exactly one match is found and the file exists, use it as `plan_source: explicit`. If multiple plan paths appear, treat as ambiguous — demote to `plan_source: inferred` for the most recent match that exists on disk, or skip if none exist or none clearly relate to the PR title/intent. Always verify the selected file exists before using it — stale or copied plan links in PR descriptions are common.
3. **Auto-discover.** Extract 2-3 keywords from the branch name (e.g., `feat/onboarding-skill` -> `onboarding`, `skill`). Glob `<root>/plans/*` and filter filenames containing those keywords. If exactly one match, use it. If multiple matches or the match looks ambiguous (e.g., generic keywords like `review`, `fix`, `update` that could hit many plans), **skip auto-discovery** — a wrong plan is worse than no plan. If zero matches, skip.

**Confidence tagging:** Record how the plan was found:
- `plan:` argument -> `plan_source: explicit` (high confidence)
- Single unambiguous PR body match -> `plan_source: explicit` (high confidence)
- Multiple/ambiguous PR body matches -> `plan_source: inferred` (lower confidence)
- Auto-discover with single unambiguous match -> `plan_source: inferred` (lower confidence)

If a plan is found, classify by contents before extraction (see "Plan Requirements Completeness" above): treat a Product Contract without implementation planning as product intent only — it must not drive implementation-unit completeness findings. Then read its **Requirements** in this order — unified `Product Contract` -> `### Requirements`, then legacy top-level `## Requirements`, then legacy `## Requirements Trace` — and the R-IDs (R1, R2, etc.) listed there, plus **Implementation Units** (current numeric subsections such as `### U1.`, `### U2.`, or `### Unit 1:` under `## Implementation Units`; legacy bullet or checkbox unit entries under that section also count). For HTML unified plans the same section names and R-/U-IDs appear as visible headings/anchors — match on the section name, ignoring HTML wrapper tags. Store the extracted requirements list and `plan_source` for Stage 6. Do not block the review if no plan is found — requirements verification is additive, not required.

When the discovered plan's Key Technical Decisions carry `session-settled:` annotations (classes `user-directed` / `user-approved`), extract each labeled KTD — the decision, its class, and the rejected alternative — for your own use in Stage 5 triage (step 6c). Settlement annotations are **context for you (the orchestrator) only**: exclude them from the Stage 2 intent summary and from every reviewer's prompt, including the cross-model adversarial pass. The point is reviewer independence: each reviewer must stay free to re-derive the rejected alternative on the merits, and you triage any settlement conflicts afterwards in Stage 5.

### Stage 2c: Keep grounding review-specific

Use the project's active instructions already in context plus the current diff and source. Give each reviewer only the context relevant to its review focus; the `project-standards` reviewer reads the actual standards sources. If a reviewer cannot scope the affected area from the diff and supplied context, allow one targeted probe.

In `pr-remote` / `branch-remote`, current source and any targeted probe must use `git show` against the supplied reviewed head ref, or the supplied diff hunks when no head ref is available; never inspect workspace paths.

 succeeded in 299ms:
"""설비 정지 시점까지 그 설비를 PASS한 제품 중 어디까지 의심할지 뽑는 분석. 제품 상태는 바꾸지 않는다."""

from collections import Counter
import csv
from pathlib import Path


METHODS = ("window", "last_10", "last_20", "lot", "since_restart")
CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS = ("stop_key", "equipment", "method", "row_index", "seq", "product_id", "lot", "final_state")


def _passes(records):
    return [record for record in records if record.result == "PASS"]


def stop_scopes(analysis):
    """정지 순서대로 (stop_key, 정지, 방식, 최초 PASS 기록들)을 돌려준다."""
    products = analysis.process.products
    lot_tracking = analysis.process.equipment_book.lot_tracking
    numbers = Counter()
    scopes = []
    for stop in analysis.stop_history:
        numbers[stop.equipment] += 1
        # 허용된 같은 설비의 재가동만 구간을 나눈다.
        start = max((item.row_index for item in analysis.restarts
                     if item.equipment == stop.equipment and item.row_index < stop.row_index), default=0)
        records = [record for record in analysis.first_inspections
                   if record.equipment == stop.equipment and start < record.row_index <= stop.row_index]
        chosen = {
            "window": _passes(stop.window),
            "last_10": _passes(records[-10:]),
            "last_20": _passes(records[-20:]),
            "since_restart": _passes(records),
        }
        if lot_tracking:
            lots = {products[item.product_id].lot for item in stop.fail_causes}
            chosen["lot"] = [record for record in _passes(records) if products[record.product_id].lot in lots]
        key = stop.equipment + "#" + str(numbers[stop.equipment])
        scopes.extend((key, stop, method, chosen[method]) for method in METHODS if method in chosen)
    return scopes


def build_containment(analysis):
    products = analysis.process.products
    return [dict(zip(CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS, (
        key, stop.equipment, method, record.row_index, record.seq, record.product_id,
        products[record.product_id].lot, products[record.product_id].status,
    ))) for key, stop, method, records in stop_scopes(analysis) for record in records]


def run_scopes(analysis):
    """방식별로 한 실행의 모든 정지에서 고른 고유 제품 합집합."""
    methods = {}
    for _, _, method, records in stop_scopes(analysis):
        methods.setdefault(method, set()).update(record.product_id for record in records)
    return methods


def write_containment(stem, rows):
    path = Path("reports") / (str(stem) + "_containment.csv")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    return path


def print_containment(rows, path):
    print("[격리 범위]")
    print("stop_key | method | product_count")
    for (key, method), count in Counter((row["stop_key"], row["method"]) for row in rows).items():
        print(key + " | " + method + " | " + str(count))
    print()
    print("저장 파일: " + path.as_posix())

+def containment_metrics(runs):
+    """runs는 (유출 집합, {방식: 고유 제품 합집합}) 목록이다. 실행별 비율은 평균하지 않고 전체를 합산한다."""
+    count = len(runs)
+    leaks = sum(len(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    methods = {}
+    for method in METHODS:
+        if not any(method in scopes for _, scopes in runs):
+            continue
+        chosen = [(leaked, scopes.get(method, set())) for leaked, scopes in runs]
+        caught = sum(len(leaked & scope) for leaked, scope in chosen)
+        total = sum(len(scope) for _, scope in chosen)
+        methods[method] = {
+            "capture_rate": Fraction(caught, leaks) if leaks else None,
+            "mean_quarantine": Fraction(total, count),
+            "precision": Fraction(caught, total) if total else None,
+            "mean_residual": Fraction(leaks - caught, count),
+        }
+    return methods
+
+
+def sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs):
+    return leaks >= MIN_LEAKS and leak_runs >= MIN_LEAK_RUNS
+
+
+def capture_met(metrics):
+    return metrics["capture_rate"] is not None and metrics["capture_rate"] >= MIN_CAPTURE
+
+
+def select_method(method_metrics, leaks, leak_runs):
+    """(선택 방식, 표시 문구). 후보가 없으면 완화하지 않는다."""
+    if not sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs):
+        return None, LOW_SAMPLE
+    candidates = [method for method, metrics in method_metrics.items() if capture_met(metrics)]
+    if not candidates:
+        return None, NO_METHOD
+    return min(candidates, key=lambda method: (method_metrics[method]["mean_quarantine"], METHODS.index(method))), "선택"
+
+
+def containment_verdict(metrics, leaks, leak_runs):
+    return "확인" if sample_sufficient(leaks, leak_runs) and capture_met(metrics) else "미확인"
+
+
+def read_selection(path, config_sha):
+    """개발 보고서의 선택을 읽는다. 설정이 다르면 그 선택을 확인할 수 없다."""
+    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
+        rows = list(csv.DictReader(source))
+    sha = next((row["value"] for row in rows if row["section"] == "provenance" and row["method"] == "config_sha256"), None)
+    selection = next((row for row in rows if row["section"] == "selection"), None)
+    if selection is None:
+        raise ValueError("개발 보고서에 선택 행이 없음: " + str(path))
+    if sha != config_sha:
+        raise ValueError("개발 보고서와 설정의 SHA-256이 다름: " + str(path))
+    if selection["method"] and selection["method"] not in METHODS:
+        raise ValueError("알 수 없는 선택 방식: " + selection["method"])
+    return selection["method"] or None
+
+
+def evaluate_containment(config_path, seeds, rules, products):
+    config, content, recipe, book = load_setup(config_path)
+    if rules != [(3, 2)]:
+        raise ValueError("--containment의 규칙은 정확히 3:2여야 함")
+    if "lot_size" not in config:
+        raise ValueError("--containment에는 lot_size 설정이 필요함")
+    faulty = set(config["faulty_equipment"])
+    if len(faulty) > 1:
+        raise ValueError("고장 설비가 여러 대인 검출률 단위는 명세에서 정해지지 않음: " + ", ".join(sorted(faulty)))
+    ruled = replace(book, window=3, fails=2)
+    runs, stops = [], 0
+    for seed in seeds:
+        generator, _, rows, _ = build_rows(seed, products, config, recipe.models, ruled)
+        analysis = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, ruled)
+        stops += len(analysis.stop_history)
+        runs.append((set(generator.leaked_products), run_scopes(analysis)))
+    provenance = {
+        "config_sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), "recipe_sha256": recipe.sha256,
+        "equipment_sha256": book.sha256, "generator_version": generator_version(config),
+        "seeds": str(seeds[0]) + "-" + str(seeds[-1]), "products": str(products),
+    }
+    return runs, stops, provenance
+
+
+def containment_rows(runs, stops, provenance, holdout, selected):
+    leaks = sum(len(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    leak_runs = sum(bool(leaked) for leaked, _ in runs)
+    metrics = containment_metrics(runs)
+    blank = dict.fromkeys(CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS, "")
+    rows = [{**blank, "section": "notice", "value": ASSUMPTION_NOTICE}]
+    rows.extend({**blank, "section": "provenance", "method": key, "value": value} for key, value in provenance.items())
+    for method, item in metrics.items():
+        rows.append({
+            **blank, "section": "method", "method": method, "runs": len(runs), "leak_runs": leak_runs, "leaks": leaks,
+            "stops": stops, "capture_rate": _decimal(item["capture_rate"], 4),
+            "mean_quarantine": _decimal(item["mean_quarantine"], 2), "precision": _decimal(item["precision"], 4),
+            "mean_residual": _decimal(item["mean_residual"], 2),
+        })
+    if holdout:
+        verdict = containment_verdict(metrics[selected], leaks, leak_runs) if selected else NO_SELECTION
+        rows.append({**blank, "section": "holdout_verdict", "method": selected or "", "value": verdict})
+    else:
+        method, text = select_method(metrics, leaks, leak_runs)
+        rows.append({**blank, "section": "selection", "method": method or "", "value": text})
+    return rows
+
+
+def print_containment_rows(rows):
+    columns = CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS[1:-1]
+    print(" | ".join(columns))
+    for row in rows:
+        if row["section"] == "method":
+            print(" | ".join(str(row[column]) for column in columns))
+    for row in rows:
+        if row["section"] in ("notice", "selection", "holdout_verdict"):
+            print((row["method"] + " " if row["method"] else "") + row["value"])
+
+

+def check_lot_config(config, book):
+    def unit(value):
+        return type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value) and 0 <= value <= 1
+
+    lot_keys = {"defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"} & config.keys()
+    if "lot_size" not in config:
+        if book.lot_tracking:
+            raise ValueError("lot_tracking 설비 파일에는 lot_size 설정이 필요함")
+        if lot_keys or "faulty_detection_loss" in config:
+            raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss와 결함 로트 설정에는 lot_size가 필요함")
+        return
+    if type(config["lot_size"]) is not int or config["lot_size"] < 1:
+        raise ValueError("lot_size는 양의 정수여야 함 (bool 불가)")
+    if not book.lot_tracking:
+        raise ValueError("lot_size 설정에는 lot_tracking 설비 파일이 필요함")
+    if lot_keys and len(lot_keys) != 2:
+        raise ValueError("defective_lot_probability와 defective_in_lot_probability는 함께 있어야 함")
+    if not all(unit(config[key]) for key in lot_keys):
+        raise ValueError("결함 로트 확률은 0–1 값이어야 함")
+    loss = config.get("faulty_detection_loss", {})
+    if not isinstance(loss, dict) or not all(unit(value) for value in loss.values()):
+        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss는 설비별 0–1 값이어야 함")
+    if not set(loss) <= set(config["faulty_equipment"]):
+        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss의 설비는 faulty_equipment에 있어야 함")
+
+
+def generator_version(config):
+    if "lot_size" in config:
+        return LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION
+    return RESTART_GENERATOR_VERSION if "restart" in config else GENERATOR_VERSION

+        parser.error("--containment는 설비 모드에서만 사용 가능")
     if args.recipe is not None:
-        return run_recipe(args.input_csv, args.recipe, parser, args.equipment, summary=args.summary, trace=args.trace)
+        return run_recipe(args.input_csv, args.recipe, parser, args.equipment, summary=args.summary, trace=args.trace,
+                          containment=args.containment)
     try:
         reports = build_reports(read_events(args.input_csv))
         paths = write_reports(args.input_csv.stem, reports)
import copy
import csv
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

from src.containment import build_containment, run_scopes
from src.equipment import load_equipment
from src.evaluate import (
    containment_metrics, containment_verdict, parse_rules, read_selection, select_method, evaluate_containment,
)
from src.generate import LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION, build_rows, generate, load_setup
from src.recipe import load_recipe
from src.recipe_rules import LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RecipeProcess
from src.summary import build_analysis


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes" / "lamp_models_retest.json"
LOT_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment_lot.json"
PLAIN_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment.json"
LOT_CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_lot.json"
CLUSTERED_CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_lot_clustered.json"
SAMPLE = ROOT / "data" / "sample_events_equipment.csv"


def row(seq, product_id, name, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment="", lot=""):
    return dict(zip(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), product_id, name, inspection, result, model, equipment, lot)))


def prepared(lots):
    rows = []
    for product_id, lot in lots.items():
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "REGISTER", model="B", lot=lot))
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "P1"))
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "P2"))
    return rows


def leak(seq, product_id, result, equipment="LEAK-1"):
    return row(seq, product_id, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)


def restart(seq, equipment="LEAK-1", result="NO_FAULT", lot=""):
    return row(seq, "", "EQUIP_RESTART", result=result, equipment=equipment, lot=lot)


def cli(directory, *arguments, module="src.main"):
    return subprocess.run(["py", "-m", module, *arguments], cwd=directory, capture_output=True,
                          env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})


def rows_of(method_rows, key, method):
    return [item["product_id"] for item in method_rows if item["stop_key"] == key and item["method"] == method]


class Base(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
        cls.book = load_equipment(LOT_EQUIPMENT, cls.models)

    def analysis(self, events):
        return build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book)


class InputTests(Base):
    def test_t67_register_stores_lot(self):
        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
        decision = process.apply(row(1, "z", "REGISTER", model="B", lot="L1"))
        self.assertTrue(decision.accepted)
        self.assertEqual(process.products["z"].lot, "L1")

    def test_t68_invalid_lot_rows_change_nothing(self):
        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
        process.apply(row(1, "a", "REGISTER", model="B", lot="L1"))
        cases = (
            (row(2, "b", "REGISTER", model="B"), "lot이 비어 있거나 형식이 잘못됨"),
            (row(2, "b", "REGISTER", model="B", lot="l-1"), "lot이 비어 있거나 형식이 잘못됨"),
            (row(2, "a", "P1", lot="L1"), "REGISTER 이외의 lot은 빈칸이어야 함"),
            (restart(2, lot="L1"), "REGISTER 이외의 lot은 빈칸이어야 함"),
            (restart(2, result="OK", lot="L1"), "REGISTER 이외의 lot은 빈칸이어야 함"),
            (row(2, "a", "REGISTER", model="A", lot="L2"), "이미 등록된 제품"),
        )
        for item, reason in cases:
            with self.subTest(reason=reason):
                before = copy.deepcopy((process.products, process.equipment))
                decision = process.apply(item)
                self.assertEqual((decision.accepted, decision.rule_id, decision.reason), (False, "RULE_PRESERVE", reason))
                self.assertEqual((process.products, process.equipment), before)
        self.assertEqual(process.products["a"].lot, "L1")

    def test_t69_mode_and_header_combinations(self):
        data = json.loads(PLAIN_EQUIPMENT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            base = Path(directory)
            arguments = ("--recipe", str(RECIPE), "--equipment")
            off = base / "off.json"
            off.write_text(json.dumps({**data, "lot_tracking": False}), encoding="utf-8")
            one, two = base / "one", base / "two"
            one.mkdir(), two.mkdir()
            self.assertEqual(cli(one, str(SAMPLE), *arguments, str(PLAIN_EQUIPMENT)).returncode, 0)
            self.assertEqual(cli(two, str(SAMPLE), *arguments, str(off)).returncode, 0)
            for path in sorted((one / "reports").iterdir()):
                self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), (two / "reports" / path.name).read_bytes(), path.name)

            eight = base / "eight.csv"
            eight.write_text(",".join(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
            self.assertEqual(cli(base, str(eight), *arguments, str(PLAIN_EQUIPMENT)).returncode, 2)
            self.assertEqual(cli(base, str(SAMPLE), *arguments, str(LOT_EQUIPMENT)).returncode, 2)
            bad = base / "bad.json"
            bad.write_text(json.dumps({**data, "lot_tracking": 1}), encoding="utf-8")
            self.assertEqual(cli(base, str(SAMPLE), *arguments, str(bad)).returncode, 3)


class ScopeTests(Base):
    def t70(self, lots="LLLLLL"):
        names = "abcdef"
        events = prepared(dict(zip(names, lots)))
        for index, (product_id, result) in enumerate(zip(names, ("PASS",) * 4 + ("FAIL",) * 2)):
            events.append(leak(100 + index, product_id, result))
        return events

    def test_t70_methods(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70()))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "window"), ["d"])
        for method in ("last_10", "last_20", "since_restart"):
            self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", method), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t71_restart_splits_the_segment(self):
        events = self.t70() + prepared({"g": "M", "h": "M", "i": "M"})
        events += [restart(200), leak(201, "g", "PASS"), leak(202, "h", "FAIL"), leak(203, "i", "FAIL")]
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(events))
        for method in ("window", "last_10", "last_20", "since_restart"):
            self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#2", method), ["g"])
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t72_lot_scope_uses_fail_lots(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70(("L1", "L1", "L2", "L2", "L1", "L2"))))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "lot"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t73_lot_scope_can_be_empty(self):
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(self.t70(("L9",) * 4 + ("L1", "L1"))))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "lot"), [])
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ["a", "b", "c", "d"])

    def test_t74_empty_and_mode_cases(self):
        events = prepared({"a": "L", "b": "L"}) + [leak(100, "a", "FAIL"), leak(101, "b", "FAIL")]
        self.assertEqual(build_containment(self.analysis(events)), [])
        self.assertEqual(build_containment(self.analysis(prepared({"a": "L"}) + [leak(100, "a", "PASS")])), [])
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            result = cli(directory, str(SAMPLE), "--recipe", str(RECIPE), "--containment")
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
            result = cli(directory, str(SAMPLE), "--recipe", str(RECIPE), "--equipment", str(PLAIN_EQUIPMENT), "--containment")
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
            path = Path(directory) / "reports" / "sample_events_equipment_containment.csv"
            with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
                self.assertNotIn("lot", [item["method"] for item in csv.DictReader(source)])

    def twelve(self):
        ids = ["p" + str(index).zfill(2) for index in range(1, 13)]
        events = prepared({name: "L" for name in ids + ["x", "y"]})
        events += [leak(100 + index, name, "PASS") for index, name in enumerate(ids)]
        return ids, events + [leak(200, "x", "FAIL"), leak(201, "y", "FAIL")]

    def test_t75_last_n_counts_recent_inspections(self):
        ids, events = self.twelve()
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(events))
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "last_10"), ids[4:])
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "last_20"), ids)
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ids)

    def test_t76_retest_pass_excluded_and_other_hold_included(self):
        events = prepared({"a": "L", "b": "L", "c": "L", "d": "L"}) + [
            leak(100, "a", "FAIL"), row(101, "a", "RETEST_REQUEST", "LEAK"), leak(102, "a", "PASS", "LEAK-2"),
            leak(103, "b", "PASS", "LEAK-2"), row(104, "b", "P3"), row(105, "b", "P4"),
            row(106, "b", "INSPECT", "LIGHT", "FAIL", equipment="LIGHT-1"),
            leak(107, "c", "FAIL", "LEAK-2"), leak(108, "d", "FAIL", "LEAK-2"),
        ]
        rows = build_containment(self.analysis(events))
        scoped = [item for item in rows if item["stop_key"] == "LEAK-2#1" and item["method"] == "since_restart"]
        self.assertEqual([(item["product_id"], item["final_state"]) for item in scoped], [("b", "보류")])

    def test_t82_leaks_inside_and_outside_last_10(self):
        _, events = self.twelve()
        scopes = run_scopes(self.analysis(events))
        result = containment_metrics([({"p01", "p12"}, scopes)])
        self.assertEqual(result["last_10"]["capture_rate"], 0.5)
        self.assertEqual(result["last_10"]["mean_residual"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result["since_restart"]["capture_rate"], 1)

    def test_t86_only_allowed_restart_of_same_equipment_splits(self):
        events = prepared({name: "L" for name in "abcdefg"}) + [
            leak(100, "e", "FAIL", "LEAK-2"), leak(101, "f", "FAIL", "LEAK-2"),
            leak(102, "a", "PASS"), restart(103, "LEAK-2"), restart(104, "LEAK-1"),
            leak(105, "b", "PASS"), leak(106, "c", "FAIL"), leak(107, "d", "FAIL"),
        ]
        analysis = self.analysis(events)
        restarts = [item for item in analysis.decisions if item.input_text.startswith("EQUIP_RESTART")]
        self.assertEqual([item.accepted for item in restarts], [True, False])
        rows = build_containment(analysis)
        self.assertEqual(rows_of(rows, "LEAK-1#1", "since_restart"), ["a", "b"])


class MetricTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t77_totals_not_mean_of_run_ratios(self):
        first = set(range(1, 10))
        result = containment_metrics([(first, {"window": first}), ({"x"}, {"window": set()})])["window"]
        self.assertEqual(float(result["capture_rate"]), 0.9)
        self.assertEqual(float(result["mean_residual"]), 0.5)

    def test_t78_precision_quarantine_residual(self):
        result = containment_metrics([({"p", "q", "r"}, {"lot": {"p", "q", "s", "t"}})])["lot"]
        self.assertEqual(round(float(result["capture_rate"]), 4), 0.6667)
        self.assertEqual(float(result["precision"]), 0.5)
        self.assertEqual(float(result["mean_quarantine"]), 4)
        self.assertEqual(float(result["mean_residual"]), 1)

    def item(self, capture, quarantine):
        from fractions import Fraction
        return {"capture_rate": Fraction(*capture), "mean_quarantine": Fraction(quarantine)}

    def test_t79_selection(self):
        pick = lambda methods, leaks=100, runs=50: select_method(methods, leaks, runs)
        self.assertEqual(pick({"window": self.item((8999, 10000), 1), "since_restart": self.item((9, 10), 50)})[0],
                         "since_restart")
        self.assertEqual(pick({"last_10": self.item((9, 10), 5), "since_restart": self.item((1, 1), 50)})[0], "last_10")
        self.assertEqual(pick({"last_10": self.item((1, 1), 5), "window": self.item((1, 1), 5)})[0], "window")
        self.assertEqual(pick({"window": self.item((1, 2), 5)}), (None, "기준 충족 방식 없음"))
        self.assertEqual(pick({"window": self.item((1, 1), 5)}, 29, 50), (None, "평가 표본 부족"))
        self.assertEqual(pick({"window": self.item((1, 1), 5)}, 100, 19), (None, "평가 표본 부족"))

    def test_t80_holdout_verdict(self):
        self.assertEqual(containment_verdict(self.item((91, 100), 5), 100, 50), "확인")
        self.assertEqual(containment_verdict(self.item((89, 100), 5), 100, 50), "미확인")
        self.assertEqual(containment_verdict({"capture_rate": None}, 0, 0), "미확인")
        self.assertEqual(containment_verdict(self.item((91, 100), 5), 100, 19), "미확인")


class GeneratorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
        cls.book = load_equipment(LOT_EQUIPMENT, cls.models)

    def test_t81_reproducible_and_truth_stays_out_of_csv(self):
        for seed, products in ((42, 500), (7, 200)):
            with self.subTest(seed=seed), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                one, two = Path(directory) / "one.csv", Path(directory) / "two.csv"
                meta = generate(seed, products, one, LOT_CONFIG)
                generate(seed, products, two, LOT_CONFIG)
                self.assertEqual(one.read_bytes(), two.read_bytes())
                self.assertEqual(meta["generator_version"], LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION)
                self.assertEqual(meta["lot_size"], 20)
                self.assertLessEqual(set(meta["leaked_products"]), set(meta["defective_products"]))
                with one.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
                    reader = csv.DictReader(source)
                    rows = list(reader)
                self.assertEqual(tuple(reader.fieldnames), LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS)
                analysis = build_analysis(rows, self.models, self.book)
                self.assertEqual(sum(not item.accepted for item in analysis.decisions), sum(meta["injected_errors"].values()))

    def test_t81_seeds_produce_leaks(self):
        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(LOT_CONFIG)
        self.assertGreater(sum(len(build_rows(seed, 500, config, recipe.models, book)[0].leaked_products)
                               for seed in range(30)), 0)

    def defect_lot_share(self, path):
        config, _, recipe, book = load_setup(path)
        defective, lots = 0, set()
        for seed in range(30):
            generator = build_rows(seed, 500, config, recipe.models, book)[0]
            defective += len(generator.defective_products)
            lots |= {(seed, (int(item[4:]) - 1) // 20) for item in generator.defective_products}
        return defective / (30 * 500), len(lots) / (30 * 25)

    def test_t83_clustered_defects(self):
        rate, share = self.defect_lot_share(CLUSTERED_CONFIG)
        self.assertTrue(0.03 <= rate <= 0.07, rate)
        self.assertLessEqual(share, 0.50)
        self.assertLess(share, self.defect_lot_share(LOT_CONFIG)[1])

    def test_t87_invalid_settings(self):
        config = json.loads(LOT_CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        config.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(LOT_EQUIPMENT))
        cases = (
            {"lot_size": 0}, {"lot_size": True}, {"faulty_detection_loss": {"LEAK-2": 1.5}},
            {"faulty_detection_loss": {"LEAK-1": 0.5}}, {"lot_size": None},
            {"defective_lot_probability": 0.25}, {"equipment": str(ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment_restart.json")},
        )
        for change in cases:
            with self.subTest(change=change), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                data = {**config, **change}
                if data["lot_size"] is None:
                    del data["lot_size"]
                path = Path(directory) / "config.json"
                path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
                output = Path(directory) / "gen.csv"
                result = cli(directory, "--seed", "1", "--products", "5", "--config", str(path), "--out", str(output),
                             module="src.generate")
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
                self.assertFalse(output.exists())


class MetaTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t88_leaked_products_validation(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            path = Path(directory) / "gen.csv"
            meta = generate(1, 30, path, LOT_CONFIG)
            base = {**meta, "defective_products": ["GEN_0001"], "leaked_products": ["GEN_0001"]}
            cases = (
                {"leaked_products": [""]}, {"leaked_products": ["GEN_0001", "GEN_0001"]},
                {"leaked_products": ["GEN_0002"]}, {"defective_products": None},
                {"defective_products": ["GEN_9999"], "leaked_products": ["GEN_9999"]},
            )
            arguments = (str(path), "--recipe", str(RECIPE), "--equipment", str(LOT_EQUIPMENT), "--summary")
            for change in cases:
                with self.subTest(change=change):
                    data = {**base, **change}
                    if data["defective_products"] is None:
                        del data["defective_products"]
                    Path(str(path) + ".meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
                    result = cli(directory, *arguments)
                    self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))


class CommandTests(unittest.TestCase):
    base = ("--containment", "--seeds", "0-1", "--products", "40")

    def test_t84_bad_commands_exit_two_without_reports(self):
        config = json.loads(LOT_CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        config.update(recipe=str(RECIPE), equipment=str(LOT_EQUIPMENT),
                      faulty_equipment={"LEAK-1": 0.1, "LEAK-2": 0.1})
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            two = Path(directory) / "two.json"
            two.write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")
            restart_config = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_restart.json"
            cases = (
                ("--config", str(LOT_CONFIG), "--rules", "3:2,5:2"), ("--config", str(LOT_CONFIG), "--rules", "5:2"),
                ("--config", str(restart_config), "--rules", "3:2"), ("--config", str(two), "--rules", "3:2"),
                ("--config", str(LOT_CONFIG), "--rules", "3:2", "--holdout"),
            )
            for case in cases:
                with self.subTest(case=case):
                    result = cli(directory, *self.base, *case, "--out", "bad", module="src.evaluate")
                    self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
                    self.assertFalse((Path(directory) / "reports").exists())

    def test_t85_same_command_same_report(self):
        arguments = (*self.base, "--config", str(LOT_CONFIG), "--rules", "3:2")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            for name in ("one", "two"):
                self.assertEqual(cli(directory, *arguments, "--out", name, module="src.evaluate").returncode, 0)
            reports = Path(directory) / "reports"
            one = (reports / "one_containment.csv").read_bytes()
            self.assertEqual(one, (reports / "two_containment.csv").read_bytes())
            self.assertIn("config_sha256", one.decode("utf-8-sig"))

    def report(self, directory, selection_rows, sha):
        path = Path(directory) / "dev.csv"
        with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=("section", "method", "value"))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerow({"section": "provenance", "method": "config_sha256", "value": sha})
            writer.writerows(selection_rows)
        return path

    def test_t80_selection_contract(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            chosen = [{"section": "selection", "method": "last_10", "value": "선택"}]
            self.assertEqual(read_selection(self.report(directory, chosen, "abc"), "abc"), "last_10")
            empty = [{"section": "selection", "method": "", "value": "평가 표본 부족"}]
            self.assertIsNone(read_selection(self.report(directory, empty, "abc"), "abc"))
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                read_selection(self.report(directory, chosen, "abc"), "other")
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                read_selection(self.report(directory, [], "abc"), "abc")

    def test_t80_no_selection_exits_zero(self):
        arguments = ("--containment", "--config", str(LOT_CONFIG), "--rules", "3:2", "--products", "40")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            result = cli(directory, *arguments, "--seeds", "0-1", "--out", "dev", module="src.evaluate")
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
            dev = Path(directory) / "reports" / "dev_containment.csv"
            self.assertIn("평가 표본 부족", dev.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
            result = cli(directory, *arguments, "--seeds", "1000-1001", "--out", "hold", "--holdout",
                         "--selected-from", str(dev), module="src.evaluate")
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))
            text = (Path(directory) / "reports" / "hold_containment.csv").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
            self.assertIn("검증할 선택 없음", text)
            self.assertNotIn("selection", text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""시드·설정·버전으로 재현하는 교육용 생산 기록 생성기."""

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import random

from .equipment import EquipmentState, load_equipment
from .recipe import load_recipe
from .recipe_rules import EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, RESTART_EVENT, RecipeProcess
from .reason_codes import INJECTED_ERROR_CODES as ERROR_KINDS


GENERATOR_VERSION = "1.1.0"
RESTART_GENERATOR_VERSION = "1.2.0"
LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION = "1.3.0"
DEFAULT_CONFIG = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "recipes/generator.json"


def event(product_id, name, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
    return dict(zip(EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS,
                    ("", product_id, name, inspection, result, model, equipment)))


class Generator:
    def __init__(self, seed, config, models, equipment_book):
        self.rng = random.Random(seed)
        self.config = config
        self.models = models
        self.book = equipment_book
        self.equipment = {name: EquipmentState() for name in self.book.equipment}
        self.cursor = defaultdict(int)
        self.defective_products = []
        self.restart_delay = restart_delay(config, equipment_book)
        self.pending_restarts = []
        self.repaired = set()
        check_lot_config(config, equipment_book)
        self.lot_size = config.get("lot_size")
        self.defective_lots = {}
        self.leaked_products = []

    def stamp(self, row):
        if self.lot_size is not None:
            row.setdefault("lot", "")
        return row

    def draw_defective(self, number):
        if "defective_lot_probability" not in self.config:
            return self.rng.random() < self.config["defective_product_probability"]
        lot = (number - 1) // self.lot_size
        if lot not in self.defective_lots:
            self.defective_lots[lot] = self.rng.random() < self.config["defective_lot_probability"]
        return self.defective_lots[lot] and self.rng.random() < self.config["defective_in_lot_probability"]

    def choose_equipment(self, inspection, avoid=""):
        names = [name for name, kind in self.book.equipment.items() if kind == inspection]
        running = [name for name in names if self.equipment[name].stopped_at_seq is None]
        preferred = [name for name in running if name != avoid] or running
        for offset in range(len(names)):
            index = (self.cursor[inspection] + offset) % len(names)
            if names[index] in preferred:
                self.cursor[inspection] = (index + 1) % len(names)
                return names[index]
        return None

    def product(self, product_id, model, number=0):
        recipe = self.models[model]
        defective = self.draw_defective(number)
        if defective:
            self.defective_products.append(product_id)
        register = event(product_id, "REGISTER", model=model)
        if self.lot_size is not None:
            register["lot"] = "LOT-" + str((number - 1) // self.lot_size + 1).zfill(4)
        yield register, False
        for step in recipe.steps:
            yield event(product_id, step), False
            for item in recipe.inspections:
                if item.after != step:
                    continue
                first_equipment = ""
                for attempt in range(recipe.retest_limit + 1):
                    equipment = self.choose_equipment(item.id, first_equipment)
                    if (equipment is None and self.restart_delay is not None
                            and item.id not in self.book.equipment.values()):
                        # 재가동으로도 생기지 않는 설비를 기다리면 생성이 끝나지 않는다.
                        raise ValueError("검사 " + item.id + "의 설비가 설비 파일에 없어 재가동 대기를 끝낼 수 없음")
                    while equipment is None and self.restart_delay is not None:
                        yield None
                        equipment = self.choose_equipment(item.id, first_equipment)
                    if equipment is None:
                        return
                    if defective:
                        probability = self.config["defective_fail_probability"]
                        if equipment not in self.repaired:
                            probability *= 1 - self.config.get("faulty_detection_loss", {}).get(equipment, 0)
                    elif equipment in self.repaired:
                        probability = self.config["normal_fail_probability"]
                    else:
                        probability = self.config["faulty_equipment"].get(
                            equipment, self.config["normal_fail_probability"])
                    result = "FAIL" if self.rng.random() < probability else "PASS"
                    if (defective and attempt == 0 and result == "PASS" and self.lot_size is not None
                            and equipment in self.config["faulty_equipment"] and equipment not in self.repaired):
                        self.leaked_products.append(product_id)
                    yield event(product_id, "INSPECT", item.id, result, equipment=equipment), attempt == 0
                    if result == "PASS":
                        break
                    if attempt == recipe.retest_limit or self.rng.random() >= self.config["retest_probability"]:
                        return
                    if attempt == 0:
                        first_equipment = equipment
                    yield event(product_id, "RETEST_REQUEST", item.id), False

    def add_restart(self, rows):
        """수리되지 않은 고장 설비만 FIXED다. 점검이 항상 맞다는 가정이며 난수를 쓰지 않는다."""
        _, name = self.pending_restarts.pop(0)
        result = "FIXED" if name in self.config["faulty_equipment"] and name not in self.repaired else "NO_FAULT"
        if result == "FIXED":
            self.repaired.add(name)
        row = self.stamp(event("", RESTART_EVENT, result=result, equipment=name))
        row["seq"] = str(len(rows) + 1)
        rows.append(row)
        self.equipment[name] = self.equipment[name].restart(result)

    def normal_events(self, products):
        names = list(self.config["model_weights"])
        weights = list(self.config["model_weights"].values())
        active = [self.product("GEN_" + str(index + 1).zfill(4),
                               self.rng.choices(names, weights=weights)[0], index + 1) for index in range(products)]
        rows = []
        while active:
            following = []
            progressed = False
            for flow in active:
                while self.pending_restarts and self.pending_restarts[0][0] <= len(rows):
                    self.add_restart(rows)
                try:
                    step = next(flow)
                except StopIteration:
                    continue
                if step is None:
                    following.append(flow)
                    continue
                row, initial = step
                self.stamp(row)
                progressed = True
                row["seq"] = str(len(rows) + 1)
                rows.append(row)
                if initial:
                    name = row["equipment"]
                    self.equipment[name] = self.equipment[name].record(row["result"], row["seq"], self.book)
                    if self.restart_delay is not None and self.equipment[name].stopped_at_seq is not None:
                        self.pending_restarts.append((len(rows) + self.restart_delay, name))
                        self.pending_restarts.sort()
                following.append(flow)
            # 대기만 남은 라운드는 행이 늘지 않으므로 가장 이른 재가동을 앞당겨 교착을 막는다.
            if following and not progressed and self.pending_restarts:
                self.add_restart(rows)
            active = following
        return rows

    def inject_errors(self, normal):
        candidates = {kind: [] for kind in ERROR_KINDS}
        process = RecipeProcess(self.models, self.book)
        for position, row in enumerate(normal, 1):
            decision = process.apply(row)
            if not decision.accepted:
                raise ValueError("생성한 정상 행이 거절됨: " + decision.reason)
            # 이전 모드에서는 EQUIP_RESTART가 합법적인 공정명이므로 재가동 모드에서만 건너뛴다.
            if self.book.restart_enabled and row["event"] == RESTART_EVENT:
                continue
            product = process.products[row["product_id"]]
            if product.discarded_inspection is not None:
                continue
            pid = row["product_id"]
            if product.status != "보류" and product.completed_steps + 1 < len(product.recipe.steps):
                future = product.recipe.steps[product.completed_steps + 1]
                candidates["SEQ_SKIP"].append((position, event(pid, future)))
            if product.model == "B" and "P5" not in product.recipe.steps:
                candidates["RECIPE_UNKNOWN_STEP"].append((position, event(pid, "P5")))
            item = product.recipe.inspections[0]
            available = [name for name, kind in self.book.equipment.items()
                         if kind == item.id and process.equipment[name].stopped_at_seq is None]
            if available:
                candidates["PRESERVE_BAD_RESULT"].append(
                    (position, event(pid, "INSPECT", item.id, "OK", equipment=available[0])))
            candidates["EQUIP_UNKNOWN"].append(
                (position, event(pid, "INSPECT", item.id, "PASS", equipment="LEAK-9")))
            for inspection in product.inspections:
                if inspection in product.open_requests:
                    continue
                names = [name for name, kind in self.book.equipment.items()
                         if kind == inspection and process.equipment[name].stopped_at_seq is None]
                if names:
                    candidates["PRESERVE_DUP_RESULT"].append(
                        (position, event(pid, "INSPECT", inspection, "PASS", equipment=names[0])))
        count = int(len(normal) * self.config["error_rate"])
        kinds = list(ERROR_KINDS)
        self.rng.shuffle(kinds)
        counts = {kind: count // len(kinds) for kind in ERROR_KINDS}
        for kind in kinds[:count % len(kinds)]:
            counts[kind] += 1
        insertions = defaultdict(list)
        for kind in ERROR_KINDS:
            if counts[kind] and not candidates[kind]:
                reason = "현재 제품·공정·검사·설비 상태에 유효한 주입 위치가 없음"
                if kind == "RECIPE_UNKNOWN_STEP" and not any(
                        row["event"] == "REGISTER" and row["model"] == "B" for row in normal):
                    reason = "B 제품 없음: B 제품의 P5 행을 주입할 수 없음"
                raise ValueError(f"오류 주입 위치 없음: {kind} (배정 {counts[kind]}건, 후보 0개; {reason})")
            for _ in range(counts[kind]):
                position, row = self.rng.choice(candidates[kind])
                insertions[position].append(self.stamp(dict(row)))
        rows = []
        for position, row in enumerate(normal, 1):
            rows.append(dict(row))
            rows.extend(insertions[position])
        for seq, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
            row["seq"] = str(seq)
        return rows, counts


def restart_delay(config, book):
    if "restart" not in config:
        return None
    restart = config["restart"]
    if not book.restart_enabled:
        raise ValueError("restart 설정에는 restart_enabled 설비 파일이 필요함")
    if (not isinstance(restart, dict) or set(restart) != {"delay_rows"}
            or type(restart["delay_rows"]) is not int or restart["delay_rows"] < 0):
        raise ValueError("restart는 0 이상의 정수 delay_rows만 가져야 함")
    return restart["delay_rows"]


def check_lot_config(config, book):
    def unit(value):
        return type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value) and 0 <= value <= 1

    lot_keys = {"defective_lot_probability", "defective_in_lot_probability"} & config.keys()
    if "lot_size" not in config:
        if book.lot_tracking:
            raise ValueError("lot_tracking 설비 파일에는 lot_size 설정이 필요함")
        if lot_keys or "faulty_detection_loss" in config:
            raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss와 결함 로트 설정에는 lot_size가 필요함")
        return
    if type(config["lot_size"]) is not int or config["lot_size"] < 1:
        raise ValueError("lot_size는 양의 정수여야 함 (bool 불가)")
    if not book.lot_tracking:
        raise ValueError("lot_size 설정에는 lot_tracking 설비 파일이 필요함")
    if lot_keys and len(lot_keys) != 2:
        raise ValueError("defective_lot_probability와 defective_in_lot_probability는 함께 있어야 함")
    if not all(unit(config[key]) for key in lot_keys):
        raise ValueError("결함 로트 확률은 0–1 값이어야 함")
    loss = config.get("faulty_detection_loss", {})
    if not isinstance(loss, dict) or not all(unit(value) for value in loss.values()):
        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss는 설비별 0–1 값이어야 함")
    if not set(loss) <= set(config["faulty_equipment"]):
        raise ValueError("faulty_detection_loss의 설비는 faulty_equipment에 있어야 함")


def generator_version(config):
    if "lot_size" in config:
        return LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION
    return RESTART_GENERATOR_VERSION if "restart" in config else GENERATOR_VERSION


def load_setup(config_path):
    """설정, 원문 SHA 계산용 바이트, 레시피, 설비를 함께 읽는다."""
    config_path = Path(config_path)
    content = config_path.read_bytes()
    config = json.loads(content.decode("utf-8-sig"))
    recipe = load_recipe(config_path.parent / config["recipe"])
    equipment = load_equipment(config_path.parent / config["equipment"], recipe.models)
    return config, content, recipe, equipment


def build_rows(seed, products, config, models, equipment):
    generator = Generator(seed, config, models, equipment)
    normal = generator.normal_events(products)
    rows, counts = generator.inject_errors(normal)
    return generator, normal, rows, counts


def generate(seed=42, products=500, out=Path("data/generated/gen_seed42.csv"), config_path=DEFAULT_CONFIG):
    if products < 1:
        raise ValueError("products는 양의 정수여야 함")
    out = Path(out)
    config, content, recipe, equipment = load_setup(config_path)
    generator, normal, rows, counts = build_rows(seed, products, config, recipe.models, equipment)
    meta = {
        "seed": seed, "products": products,
        "generator_version": generator_version(config),
        "sha256": {"config": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(),
                   "recipe": recipe.sha256, "equipment": equipment.sha256},
        "normal_rows": len(normal), "rows": len(rows), "injected_errors": counts,
        "injection_rate": config["error_rate"],
        "defective_count": len(generator.defective_products),
        "defective_products": generator.defective_products,
    }
    if "lot_size" in config:
        meta.update(lot_size=config["lot_size"], leaked_products=sorted(generator.leaked_products))
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS if equipment.lot_tracking else EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    Path(str(out) + ".meta.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return meta


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--products", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("data/generated/gen_seed42.csv"))
    parser.add_argument("--config", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_CONFIG)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        meta = generate(args.seed, args.products, args.out, args.config)
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, KeyError) as error:
        parser.exit(2, "오류: " + str(error) + "\n")
    print("생성: " + args.out.as_posix())
    print(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
