"""기존 보고서 묶음과 독립된 전체 입력 분석과 긴 표 summary."""

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from pathlib import Path

from .recipe_rules import RESTART_EVENT, RecipeProcess
from .reason_codes import INJECTED_ERROR_CODES, REASON_CODES, rejection_code


ASSUMPTION_NOTICE = "생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다."
SUMMARY_COLUMNS = (
    "section", "key", "value", "numerator", "denominator", "count", "inspection", "equipment", "rule_id",
    "first_count", "first_fail_count", "first_fail_rate", "all_count", "all_fail_count", "all_fail_rate",
    "rejected_count", "unperformed_count", "state", "row_index", "seq", "product_id",
    "cause_row_indices", "cause_seqs", "cause_product_ids", "normal_rows", "injected_count",
    "injection_rate", "actual_rejections", "reason_template",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InspectionRecord:
    # 1부터 세는 데이터 행 인덱스이며 CSV 헤더는 세지 않는다. seq와 무관하다.
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    inspection: str
    equipment: str
    result: str
    retest_number: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentStop:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    fail_causes: tuple
    window: tuple


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentRestart:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    result: str


@dataclass
class Analysis:
    process: RecipeProcess
    events: list
    decisions: list
    first_inspections: list
    all_inspections: list
    stops: dict
    stop_history: list
    restarts: list

    def stop_at(self, equipment, row_index):
        """그 행 시점에 유효했던 정지."""
        return [stop for stop in self.stop_history
                if stop.equipment == equipment and stop.row_index < row_index][-1]


def build_analysis(events, models, equipment_book):
    """입력을 물리 순서로 처리한다. 분석과 trace가 같은 판정·정지 근거를 쓴다."""
    process = RecipeProcess(models, equipment_book)
    events = [dict(event) for event in events]
    decisions, first, all_results, stops = [], [], [], {}
    stop_history, restarts = [], []
    equipment_windows = defaultdict(list)
    for row_index, event in enumerate(events, 1):
        product_before = process.products.get(event.get("product_id"))
        inspection = event.get("inspection")
        is_first = product_before is not None and inspection not in product_before.inspections
        decision = process.apply(event)
        decisions.append(decision)
        if decision.accepted and equipment_book.restart_enabled and event["event"] == RESTART_EVENT:
            equipment_windows[event["equipment"]].clear()
            restarts.append(EquipmentRestart(event["equipment"], row_index, decision.seq, event["result"]))
        if not decision.accepted or event["event"] != "INSPECT":
            continue
        product = process.products[event["product_id"]]
        record = InspectionRecord(
            row_index, decision.seq, decision.product_id, inspection, event["equipment"], event["result"],
            0 if is_first else product.retest_counts[inspection],
        )
        all_results.append(record)
        if not is_first:
            continue
        first.append(record)
        window = equipment_windows[record.equipment]
        window.append(record)
        del window[:-equipment_book.window]
        if process.equipment[record.equipment].stopped_at_seq is not None:
            stop = EquipmentStop(
                record.equipment, row_index, record.seq, record.product_id,
                tuple(item for item in window if item.result == "FAIL"), tuple(window),
            )
            stops.setdefault(record.equipment, stop)
            stop_history.append(stop)
    return Analysis(process, events, decisions, first, all_results, stops, stop_history, restarts)


def _ratio(numerator, denominator):
    if not denominator:
        return ""
    value = Decimal(numerator) / Decimal(denominator)
    return format(value.quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP), ".4f")


def _row(section, key, **fields):
    return {column: fields.get(column, section if column == "section" else key if column == "key" else "")
            for column in SUMMARY_COLUMNS}


def _metrics(section, key, first, all_results, rejected_count, unperformed_count="", **identity):
    first_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in first)
    all_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in all_results)
    return _row(
        section, key, first_count=len(first), first_fail_count=first_fails,
        first_fail_rate=_ratio(first_fails, len(first)), all_count=len(all_results),
        all_fail_count=all_fails, all_fail_rate=_ratio(all_fails, len(all_results)),
        rejected_count=rejected_count, unperformed_count=unperformed_count, **identity,
    )


def build_summary(analysis, meta=None):
    """비율 분모는 허용된 결과뿐이다. 알려지지 않은 거절 사유는 실패한다."""
    process = analysis.process
    rejected = [(event, decision) for event, decision in zip(analysis.events, analysis.decisions)
                if not decision.accepted]
    codes = Counter(rejection_code(decision, event, process.models) for event, decision in rejected)
    first, all_results = analysis.first_inspections, analysis.all_inspections
    products = list(process.products.values())
    inspection_ids = sorted({item.id for model in process.models.values() for item in model.inspections})
    unperformed = Counter(item.id for product in products for item in product.recipe.inspections
                          if item.id not in product.inspections)
    rejected_inspections = [event for event, _ in rejected if event.get("event") == "INSPECT"]
    rows = [_row("notice", "generation_assumption", value=ASSUMPTION_NOTICE)]

    first_results = {(record.product_id, record.inspection): record.result for record in first}
    first_pass = sum(product.status == "레시피 모의 흐름 완료" and all(
        first_results.get((product_id, item.id)) == "PASS" for item in product.recipe.inspections
    ) for product_id, product in process.products.items())
    rows.extend((
        _row("product_metrics", "registered_count", value=len(products)),
        _row("product_metrics", "first_pass_count", value=first_pass),
        _row("product_metrics", "fpy", value=_ratio(first_pass, len(products)), numerator=first_pass,
             denominator=len(products)),
    ))
    statuses = Counter("완료" if product.status == "레시피 모의 흐름 완료" else
                       "공정 진행 중" if product.status.startswith("공정 진행 중(") else product.status
                       for product in products)
    for state in ("완료", "보류", "폐기", "검사 대기", "공정 진행 중", "등록"):
        rows.append(_row("status_distribution", state, count=statuses[state]))

    rows.append(_metrics("inspection_metrics", "ALL", first, all_results,
                         len(rejected_inspections), sum(unperformed.values())))
    for inspection in sorted(set(inspection_ids) | {event.get("inspection") or "" for event in rejected_inspections}):
        rows.append(_metrics(
            "inspection_metrics", inspection,
            [item for item in first if item.inspection == inspection],
            [item for item in all_results if item.inspection == inspection],
            sum((event.get("inspection") or "") == inspection for event in rejected_inspections), unperformed[inspection],
            inspection=inspection,
        ))
    equipment_ids = set(process.equipment) | {event.get("equipment") or "" for event in rejected_inspections}
    for equipment in sorted(equipment_ids):
        rows.append(_metrics(
            "equipment_metrics", equipment,
            [item for item in first if item.equipment == equipment],
            [item for item in all_results if item.equipment == equipment],
            sum((event.get("equipment") or "") == equipment for event in rejected_inspections),
            inspection=process.equipment_book.equipment.get(equipment, ""), equipment=equipment,
"""제품별 선형 레시피 판정. 파일과 터미널 입출력은 하지 않는다."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
import re
from typing import Dict, FrozenSet, Mapping, Optional, Tuple

from .recipe import ModelRecipe
from .equipment import EquipmentBook, EquipmentState


INPUT_COLUMNS = ("seq", "product_id", "event", "inspection", "result", "model")
EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS = INPUT_COLUMNS + ("equipment",)
LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS = EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS + ("lot",)
RULE_IDS = (
    "RULE_RECIPE",
    "RULE_SEQ",
    "RULE_INSP_PRE",
    "RULE_NOT_DONE",
    "RULE_FAIL_HOLD",
    "RULE_PRESERVE",
)
RESTART_EVENT = "EQUIP_RESTART"
RESTART_RESULTS = ("FIXED", "NO_FAULT")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InspectionAttempt:
    seq: str
    result: str
    retest_number: int = 0
    equipment: str = ""

    @property
    def text(self) -> str:
        suffix = "(재검사 " + str(self.retest_number) + ")" if self.retest_number else ""
        equipment = "[" + self.equipment + "]" if self.equipment else ""
        return self.result + "@" + self.seq + equipment + suffix


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecipeProduct:
    model: str
    recipe: ModelRecipe
    completed_steps: int = 0
    inspections: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    open_requests: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset()
    retest_counts: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    inspection_history: Dict[str, Tuple[InspectionAttempt, ...]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    discarded_inspection: Optional[str] = None
    lot: str = ""

    @property
    def history(self) -> str:
        return "; ".join(
            item.id + ": " + " → ".join(attempt.text for attempt in self.inspection_history[item.id])
            for item in self.recipe.inspections if item.id in self.inspection_history
        )

    @property
    def status(self) -> str:
        if self.discarded_inspection is not None:
            return "폐기"
        if "FAIL" in self.inspections.values():
            return "보류"
        if self.completed_steps == len(self.recipe.steps) and all(
            self.inspections.get(item.id) == "PASS" for item in self.recipe.inspections
        ):
            return "레시피 모의 흐름 완료"
        if self.completed_steps == 0:
            return "등록"
        if self.completed_steps < len(self.recipe.steps):
            return "공정 진행 중(" + self.recipe.steps[self.completed_steps - 1] + " 완료)"
        return "검사 대기"

    @property
    def incomplete_reason(self) -> str:
        if self.discarded_inspection is not None:
            return self.discarded_inspection + " 재검사 한도 " + str(self.recipe.retest_limit) + "회 초과 FAIL로 폐기"
        reasons = [step + " 미완료" for step in self.recipe.steps[self.completed_steps:]]
        for item in self.recipe.inspections:
            result = self.inspections.get(item.id)
            if result is None:
                reasons.append(item.id + " 미실시")
            elif result == "FAIL":
                suffix = "(재검사 요청됨)" if item.id in self.open_requests else ""
                reasons.append(item.id + " FAIL" + suffix)
        return "; ".join(reasons)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    input_text: str
    rule_id: str
    accepted: bool
    reason: str
    state: str


class RecipeProcess:
    def __init__(self, models: Mapping[str, ModelRecipe], equipment_book: Optional[EquipmentBook] = None) -> None:
        self.models = models
        self.retest_enabled = all(model.retest_limit is not None for model in models.values())
        self.products: Dict[str, RecipeProduct] = {}
        self.equipment_book = equipment_book
        self.equipment = {name: EquipmentState() for name in equipment_book.equipment} if equipment_book else {}

    def apply(self, row: Mapping[str, str]) -> Decision:
        invalid = self._invalid_fields(row)
        if invalid:
            return self._decision(row, "RULE_PRESERVE", False, invalid)
        if self._is_restart(row):
            return self._restart(row)

        product_id = row["product_id"]
        event = row["event"]
        product = self.products.get(product_id)
        if event == "REGISTER":
            if product is not None:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_PRESERVE", False, "이미 등록된 제품")
            model = row["model"]
            if model not in self.models:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_RECIPE", False, "model이 비어 있거나 레시피에 없는 모델")
            updated = RecipeProduct(model, self.models[model], lot=row.get("lot", ""))
            rule_id = "RULE_RECIPE"
        elif product is None:
            return self._decision(row, "RULE_PRESERVE", False, "미등록 제품")
        elif product.discarded_inspection is not None:
            return self._decision(row, "RULE_RETEST", False, "폐기된 제품")
        elif event in ("INSPECT", "RETEST_REQUEST"):
            if event == "RETEST_REQUEST" and not self.retest_enabled:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_RECIPE", False, "재검사 모드가 아닌 레시피")
            inspection = row["inspection"]
            item = next((item for item in product.recipe.inspections if item.id == inspection), None)
            if item is None:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_RECIPE", False, "제품 레시피에 없는 검사 " + inspection)
            if event == "RETEST_REQUEST":
                if inspection in product.open_requests:
                    return self._decision(row, "RULE_RETEST", False, "이미 열린 재검사 요청")
                if product.inspections.get(inspection) != "FAIL":
                    return self._decision(row, "RULE_RETEST", False, "미해결 FAIL 없음")
                updated = replace(product, open_requests=product.open_requests | {inspection})
                attempt = InspectionAttempt(row["seq"], "요청")
                rule_id = "RULE_RETEST"
            else:
                if self.equipment_book is not None:
                    equipment = row["equipment"]
                    if equipment not in self.equipment:
                        return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", False, "등록되지 않은 설비")
                    if self.equipment_book.equipment[equipment] != inspection:
                        return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", False, "검사 종류가 다른 설비")
                    if self.equipment[equipment].stopped_at_seq is not None:
                        return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", False, "정지된 설비")
                if product.completed_steps <= product.recipe.steps.index(item.after):
                    return self._decision(row, "RULE_INSP_PRE", False, item.after + " 미완료")
                result = row["result"]
                if inspection in product.inspections:
                    if inspection not in product.open_requests:
                        return self._decision(row, "RULE_PRESERVE", False, inspection + " 결과 이미 존재")
                    count = product.retest_counts.get(inspection, 0) + 1
                    updated = replace(
                        product, inspections={**product.inspections, inspection: result},
                        open_requests=product.open_requests - {inspection},
                        retest_counts={**product.retest_counts, inspection: count},
                        discarded_inspection=(
                            inspection if count >= product.recipe.retest_limit and result == "FAIL" else None
                        ),
                    )
                    attempt = InspectionAttempt(row["seq"], result, count, row.get("equipment", ""))
                    rule_id = "RULE_RETEST"
                else:
                    updated = replace(product, inspections={**product.inspections, inspection: result})
                    attempt = InspectionAttempt(row["seq"], result, equipment=row.get("equipment", ""))
                    rule_id = (
                        "RULE_FAIL_HOLD"
                        if updated.status in ("보류", "레시피 모의 흐름 완료")
                        else "RULE_NOT_DONE"
                    )
            if self.retest_enabled:
                updated = replace(updated, inspection_history={
                    **product.inspection_history,
                    inspection: product.inspection_history.get(inspection, ()) + (attempt,),
                })
        else:
            if event not in product.recipe.steps:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_RECIPE", False, "제품 레시피에 없는 공정 " + event)
            target = product.recipe.steps.index(event)
            if target < product.completed_steps:
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_PRESERVE", False, "이미 지난 공정 재입력")
            if product.status == "보류":
                return self._decision(row, "RULE_FAIL_HOLD", False, "FAIL로 보류 중인 제품")
            if target != product.completed_steps:
                return self._decision(
                    row, "RULE_SEQ", False, product.recipe.steps[product.completed_steps] + " 미완료"
                )
            updated = replace(product, completed_steps=product.completed_steps + 1)
            rule_id = "RULE_SEQ"

        if self.equipment_book is not None and event == "INSPECT" and row["inspection"] not in product.inspections:
            equipment = row["equipment"]
            self.equipment[equipment] = self.equipment[equipment].record(row["result"], row["seq"], self.equipment_book)
        self.products[product_id] = updated
        return self._decision(row, rule_id, True, "")

    def _is_restart(self, row: Mapping[str, str]) -> bool:
        return (self.equipment_book is not None and self.equipment_book.restart_enabled
                and row.get("event") == RESTART_EVENT)

    def _restart(self, row: Mapping[str, str]) -> Decision:
        equipment = row["equipment"]
        if equipment not in self.equipment:
            return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", False, "등록되지 않은 설비")
        if self.equipment[equipment].stopped_at_seq is None:
            return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", False, "가동 중인 설비 재가동")
        self.equipment[equipment] = self.equipment[equipment].restart(row["result"])
        return self._decision(row, "RULE_EQUIP", True, "")

    def _invalid_fields(self, row: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
        columns = INPUT_COLUMNS
        if self.equipment_book is not None:
            columns = LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS if self.equipment_book.lot_tracking else EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS
        if set(row) != set(columns) or any(
            not isinstance(row.get(name), str) for name in columns
        ):
            return "CSV 열 수 또는 값 누락"
        seq = row["seq"]
        if not seq.isascii() or not seq.isdigit() or int(seq) <= 0:
            return "seq는 양의 정수여야 함"
        if self.equipment_book is not None and self.equipment_book.lot_tracking:
            if row["event"] != "REGISTER":
                if row["lot"]:
                    return "REGISTER 이외의 lot은 빈칸이어야 함"
            elif re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z0-9_-]+", row["lot"]) is None:
                return "lot이 비어 있거나 형식이 잘못됨"
        if self._is_restart(row):
            if row["product_id"]:
                return "EQUIP_RESTART의 product_id는 빈칸이어야 함"
            if row["inspection"] or row["model"]:
                return "EQUIP_RESTART의 inspection과 model은 빈칸이어야 함"
            if row["result"] not in RESTART_RESULTS:
                return "잘못된 재가동 결과 (FIXED 또는 NO_FAULT 필요)"
            return "" if row["equipment"] else "equipment가 비어 있음"
        if not row["product_id"].strip():
            return "product_id가 비어 있음"
        if not row["event"]:
            return "event가 비어 있음"
        if row["event"] != "REGISTER" and row["model"]:
            return "REGISTER 이외의 model은 빈칸이어야 함"
        if row["event"] == "INSPECT":
            if not row["inspection"]:
                return "inspection이 비어 있음"
            if row["result"] not in ("PASS", "FAIL"):
                return "잘못된 result (PASS 또는 FAIL 필요)"
        elif row["event"] == "RETEST_REQUEST":
            if not row["inspection"]:
                return "inspection이 비어 있음"
            if row["result"]:
                return "RETEST_REQUEST의 result는 빈칸이어야 함"
        elif row["inspection"] or row["result"]:
            return "INSPECT 이외의 inspection과 result는 빈칸이어야 함"
        if self.equipment_book is not None:
            if row["event"] == "INSPECT" and not row["equipment"]:
                return "equipment가 비어 있음"
            if row["event"] != "INSPECT" and row["equipment"]:
                return "INSPECT 이외의 equipment는 빈칸이어야 함"
        return ""

    def _decision(self, row: Mapping[str, str], rule_id: str, accepted: bool, reason: str) -> Decision:
        product_id = str(row.get("product_id") or "")
        product = self.products.get(product_id)
        equipment = ""
        if self.equipment_book is not None and row.get("event") == "INSPECT" and row.get("equipment"):
            equipment = " @" + str(row["equipment"])
        if self._is_restart(row):
            name = row.get("equipment")
            input_text = " ".join(str(row[key]) for key in ("event", "inspection", "result") if row.get(key))
            return Decision(
                seq=str(row.get("seq") or ""), product_id=product_id,
                input_text=input_text + (" @" + str(name) if name else ""), rule_id=rule_id, accepted=accepted,
                reason=reason, state="설비 " + self.equipment[name].state if name in self.equipment else "미등록",
            )
        return Decision(
            seq=str(row.get("seq") or ""),
            product_id=product_id,
            input_text=" ".join(
                str(row.get(name) or "") for name in ("event", "inspection", "result")
            ).rstrip() + equipment,
            rule_id=rule_id,
            accepted=accepted,
            reason=reason,
            state=product.status if product is not None else "미등록",
        )
"""CSV 입력, 터미널 출력과 CSV 보고서 저장."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Sequence

from .rules import INPUT_COLUMNS, RULE_IDS, Process


ROW_COLUMNS = ("seq", "product_id", "input", "rule_id", "decision", "rejection_reason", "state")
PRODUCT_COLUMNS = ("product_id", "state", "incomplete_reason")
REJECTION_COLUMNS = ("rule_id", "rejection_count")


def read_events(path: Path, columns=INPUT_COLUMNS):
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
        reader = csv.DictReader(source, strict=True)
        if reader.fieldnames != list(columns):
            raise ValueError("CSV 헤더는 " + ",".join(columns) + " 이어야 함")
        return list(reader)


def build_reports(events):
    process = Process()
    rows = []
    counts = dict.fromkeys(RULE_IDS, 0)
    for event in events:
        decision = process.apply(event)
        rows.append(
            (
                decision.seq,
                decision.product_id,
                decision.input_text,
                decision.rule_id,
                "허용" if decision.accepted else "거절",
                decision.reason,
                decision.state,
            )
        )
        if not decision.accepted:
            counts[decision.rule_id] += 1
    products = [
        (product_id, product.status, product.incomplete_reason)
        for product_id, product in process.products.items()
    ]
    return rows, products, list(counts.items())


def write_reports(stem: str, reports, product_columns=PRODUCT_COLUMNS, *, equipment_mode=False, restart_mode=False):
    directory = Path("reports")
    directory.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    paths = []
    suffixes = ("rows", "products", "rejections")
    report_columns = (ROW_COLUMNS, product_columns, REJECTION_COLUMNS)
    if equipment_mode:
        from .recipe_reports import EQUIPMENT_COLUMNS, RESTART_COLUMNS

        suffixes += ("equipment",)
        report_columns += (RESTART_COLUMNS if restart_mode else EQUIPMENT_COLUMNS,)
    for suffix, columns, rows in zip(
        suffixes,
        report_columns,
        reports,
    ):
        path = directory / (stem + "_" + suffix + ".csv")
        with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
            writer = csv.writer(target)
            writer.writerow(columns)
            writer.writerows(rows)
        paths.append(path)
    return paths


def print_reports(reports, paths, product_heading="product_id | 최종 상태 | 미완료 이유", *, equipment_mode=False,
                  restart_mode=False):
    sections = (
        ("행별 판정", "seq | product_id | 입력 | 적용 규칙 ID | 허용/거절 | 거절 이유 | 처리 후 상태"),
        ("제품별 최종 상태", product_heading),
        ("규칙 ID별 거절 건수", "규칙 ID | 거절 건수"),
    )
    if equipment_mode:
        heading = "equipment | inspection | counted_results | fail_count | state | stopped_at_seq"
        if restart_mode:
            heading += " | stop_count | restart_count | last_restart_result"
        sections += (("설비별 최종 상태", heading),)
    for (title, heading), rows in zip(sections, reports):
        print("[" + title + "]")
        print(heading)
        for row in rows:
            print(" | ".join(str(value) for value in row))
        print()
    print("저장 파일:")
    for path in paths:
        print(path.as_posix())


def run_recipe(input_csv: Path, recipe_path: Path, parser, equipment_path=None, *, summary=False, trace=(),
               containment=False) -> int:
    from .recipe import RecipeError, load_recipe
    from .recipe_reports import (
        LOT_COLUMN_INDEX, PRODUCT_COLUMNS as RECIPE_PRODUCT_COLUMNS, RETEST_PRODUCT_COLUMNS, build_recipe_reports,
    )
    from .recipe_rules import INPUT_COLUMNS as RECIPE_INPUT_COLUMNS, EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS

    try:
        book = load_recipe(recipe_path)
    except RecipeError as error:
        parser.exit(3, "레시피 오류:\n" + "\n".join(error.errors) + "\n")
    equipment_book = None
    if equipment_path is not None:
        from .equipment import EquipmentError, load_equipment

        try:
            equipment_book = load_equipment(equipment_path, book.models)
        except EquipmentError as error:
            parser.exit(3, "설비 오류:\n" + "\n".join(error.errors) + "\n")
    print("레시피: " + recipe_path.as_posix() + " | SHA-256: " + book.sha256[:12])
    if equipment_book is not None:
        print("설비: " + equipment_path.as_posix() + " | SHA-256: " + equipment_book.sha256[:12])
    try:
        lot_mode = equipment_book is not None and equipment_book.lot_tracking
        input_columns = RECIPE_INPUT_COLUMNS
        if equipment_book is not None:
            input_columns = LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS if lot_mode else EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS
        events = read_events(input_csv, input_columns)
        reports = build_recipe_reports(events, book.models, equipment_book)
        summary_rows = None
        traces = []
        if summary or trace or containment:
            from .summary import build_analysis

            analysis = build_analysis(events, book.models, equipment_book)
        if summary:
            from .reason_codes import INJECTED_ERROR_CODES
            from .summary import build_summary

            meta_path = Path(str(input_csv) + ".meta.json")
            try:
                meta = json.loads(meta_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
                if not isinstance(meta, dict):
                    raise ValueError("meta.json은 객체여야 함")
                if type(meta.get("normal_rows")) is not int or meta["normal_rows"] < 0:
                    raise ValueError("meta.json의 normal_rows는 0 이상의 정수여야 함")
                injected = meta.get("injected_errors")
                if not isinstance(injected, dict) or any(
                        type(count) is not int or count < 0 for count in injected.values()):
                    raise ValueError("meta.json의 injected_errors는 값이 0 이상의 정수인 객체여야 함")
                if not set(injected) <= set(INJECTED_ERROR_CODES):
                    raise ValueError("meta.json의 injected_errors 키는 주입 오류 5종 코드만 허용됨")
                if "defective_products" in meta:
                    defective = meta["defective_products"]
                    if not isinstance(defective, list) or any(
                            not isinstance(product_id, str) or not product_id.strip() for product_id in defective):

import copy
import csv
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

from src.containment import build_containment, run_scopes

    containment_metrics, containment_verdict, parse_rules, read_selection, select_method, evaluate_containment,
)
from src.generate import LOT_GENERATOR_VERSION, build_rows, generate, load_setup

from src.summary import build_analysis


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes" / "lamp_models_retest.json"
LOT_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment_lot.json"
PLAIN_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment.json"
LOT_CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_lot.json"
CLUSTERED_CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_lot_clustered.json"
SAMPLE = ROOT / "data" / "sample_events_equipment.csv"


def row(seq, product_id, name, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment="", lot=""):
    return dict(zip(LOT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), product_id, name, inspection, result, model, equipment, lot)))


def prepared(lots):
    rows = []
    for product_id, lot in lots.items():
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "REGISTER", model="B", lot=lot))
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "P1"))
        rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, "P2"))
    return rows


def leak(seq, product_id, result, equipment="LEAK-1"):
    return row(seq, product_id, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)


def restart(seq, equipment="LEAK-1", result="NO_FAULT", lot=""):
    return row(seq, "", "EQUIP_RESTART", result=result, equipment=equipment, lot=lot)


def cli(directory, *arguments, module="src.main"):
    return subprocess.run(["py", "-m", module, *arguments], cwd=directory, capture_output=True,
                          env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})


def rows_of(method_rows, key, method):
    return [item["product_id"] for item in method_rows if item["stop_key"] == key and item["method"] == method]


class Base(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.models = load_recipe(RECIPE).models
        cls.book = load_equipment(LOT_EQUIPMENT, cls.models)

    def analysis(self, events):
        return build_analysis(events, self.models, self.book)


class InputTests(Base):

README.md:284:이 기준은 Western Electric 관리도 규칙의 “연속 3점 중 2점” 패턴을 PASS/FAIL에 맞게 단순화한 **교육용 가상 규칙**입니다. [NIST의 WECO 규칙 설명](https://www.itl.nist.gov/div898/handbook/pmc/section3/pmc32.htm)은 측정값이 중심선 같은 쪽의 2σ 경계를 넘는 패턴을 다룹니다(확인: 2026-10-18). 여기서는 측정값·평균·σ·관리한계를 계산하지 않습니다. **원래 통계 규칙과 동일하지 않으며, 업계 공통 설비 정지 기준이나 SL 실제 기준으로 검증된 숫자가 아닙니다.** 설비 고장을 확정하지도 않습니다.
README.md:288:### 설비 규칙과 상태 보존
README.md:314:[고정 샘플](data/sample_events_equipment.csv)은 T33–T39를 담은 94행(허용 87·거절 7), 제품 17개입니다. T33과 T35는 같은 최초 FAIL·요청·정지 이력을 공유하고, `BUFFER_` 제품의 허용 PASS로 공유 설비의 이전 창을 정리합니다. LEAK-2는 seq 25에서 누적 3건/FAIL 2건으로, LEAK-1은 seq 94에서 누적 12건/FAIL 5건으로 정지합니다. LIGHT-1·FINAL-1은 미사용·가동입니다. 거절은 `RULE_EQUIP 4`, `RULE_PRESERVE 2`, `RULE_INSP_PRE 1`이며 나머지 규칙은 0건입니다. `tests/test_equipment.py`에서 T33–T41, 모든 거절의 전체 상태 불변, 파일 오류, 기존 보고서 9개 보존과 출력 재현성을 검증합니다.

"""기존 보고서 묶음과 독립된 전체 입력 분석과 긴 표 summary."""

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from pathlib import Path

from .recipe_rules import RESTART_EVENT, RecipeProcess
from .reason_codes import INJECTED_ERROR_CODES, REASON_CODES, rejection_code


ASSUMPTION_NOTICE = "생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다."
SUMMARY_COLUMNS = (
    "section", "key", "value", "numerator", "denominator", "count", "inspection", "equipment", "rule_id",
    "first_count", "first_fail_count", "first_fail_rate", "all_count", "all_fail_count", "all_fail_rate",
    "rejected_count", "unperformed_count", "state", "row_index", "seq", "product_id",
    "cause_row_indices", "cause_seqs", "cause_product_ids", "normal_rows", "injected_count",
    "injection_rate", "actual_rejections", "reason_template",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InspectionRecord:
    # 1부터 세는 데이터 행 인덱스이며 CSV 헤더는 세지 않는다. seq와 무관하다.
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    inspection: str
    equipment: str
    result: str
    retest_number: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentStop:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    product_id: str
    fail_causes: tuple
    window: tuple


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquipmentRestart:
    equipment: str
    row_index: int
    seq: str
    result: str


@dataclass
class Analysis:
    process: RecipeProcess
    events: list
    decisions: list
    first_inspections: list
    all_inspections: list
    stops: dict
    stop_history: list
    restarts: list

    def stop_at(self, equipment, row_index):
        """그 행 시점에 유효했던 정지."""
        return [stop for stop in self.stop_history
                if stop.equipment == equipment and stop.row_index < row_index][-1]


def build_analysis(events, models, equipment_book):
    """입력을 물리 순서로 처리한다. 분석과 trace가 같은 판정·정지 근거를 쓴다."""
    process = RecipeProcess(models, equipment_book)
    events = [dict(event) for event in events]
    decisions, first, all_results, stops = [], [], [], {}
    stop_history, restarts = [], []
    equipment_windows = defaultdict(list)
    for row_index, event in enumerate(events, 1):
        product_before = process.products.get(event.get("product_id"))
        inspection = event.get("inspection")
        is_first = product_before is not None and inspection not in product_before.inspections
        decision = process.apply(event)
        decisions.append(decision)
        if decision.accepted and equipment_book.restart_enabled and event["event"] == RESTART_EVENT:
            equipment_windows[event["equipment"]].clear()
            restarts.append(EquipmentRestart(event["equipment"], row_index, decision.seq, event["result"]))
        if not decision.accepted or event["event"] != "INSPECT":
            continue
        product = process.products[event["product_id"]]
        record = InspectionRecord(
            row_index, decision.seq, decision.product_id, inspection, event["equipment"], event["result"],
            0 if is_first else product.retest_counts[inspection],
        )
        all_results.append(record)
        if not is_first:
            continue
        first.append(record)
        window = equipment_windows[record.equipment]
        window.append(record)
        del window[:-equipment_book.window]
        if process.equipment[record.equipment].stopped_at_seq is not None:
            stop = EquipmentStop(
                record.equipment, row_index, record.seq, record.product_id,
                tuple(item for item in window if item.result == "FAIL"), tuple(window),
            )
            stops.setdefault(record.equipment, stop)
            stop_history.append(stop)
    return Analysis(process, events, decisions, first, all_results, stops, stop_history, restarts)


def _ratio(numerator, denominator):
    if not denominator:
        return ""
    value = Decimal(numerator) / Decimal(denominator)
    return format(value.quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP), ".4f")


def _row(section, key, **fields):
    return {column: fields.get(column, section if column == "section" else key if column == "key" else "")
            for column in SUMMARY_COLUMNS}


def _metrics(section, key, first, all_results, rejected_count, unperformed_count="", **identity):
    first_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in first)
    all_fails = sum(item.result == "FAIL" for item in all_results)
    return _row(
        section, key, first_count=len(first), first_fail_count=first_fails,
        first_fail_rate=_ratio(first_fails, len(first)), all_count=len(all_results),
        all_fail_count=all_fails, all_fail_rate=_ratio(all_fails, len(all_results)),
        rejected_count=rejected_count, unperformed_count=unperformed_count, **identity,
    )


def build_summary(analysis, meta=None):
    """비율 분모는 허용된 결과뿐이다. 알려지지 않은 거절 사유는 실패한다."""
    process = analysis.process
    rejected = [(event, decision) for event, decision in zip(analysis.events, analysis.decisions)
                if not decision.accepted]
    codes = Counter(rejection_code(decision, event, process.models) for event, decision in rejected)
    first, all_results = analysis.first_inspections, analysis.all_inspections
    products = list(process.products.values())
    inspection_ids = sorted({item.id for model in process.models.values() for item in model.inspections})
    unperformed = Counter(item.id for product in products for item in product.recipe.inspections
                          if item.id not in product.inspections)
    rejected_inspections = [event for event, _ in rejected if event.get("event") == "INSPECT"]
    rows = [_row("notice", "generation_assumption", value=ASSUMPTION_NOTICE)]

    first_results = {(record.product_id, record.inspection): record.result for record in first}
    first_pass = sum(product.status == "레시피 모의 흐름 완료" and all(
        first_results.get((product_id, item.id)) == "PASS" for item in product.recipe.inspections
    ) for product_id, product in process.products.items())
    rows.extend((
        _row("product_metrics", "registered_count", value=len(products)),
        _row("product_metrics", "first_pass_count", value=first_pass),
        _row("product_metrics", "fpy", value=_ratio(first_pass, len(products)), numerator=first_pass,
             denominator=len(products)),
    ))
    statuses = Counter("완료" if product.status == "레시피 모의 흐름 완료" else
                       "공정 진행 중" if product.status.startswith("공정 진행 중(") else product.status
                       for product in products)
    for state in ("완료", "보류", "폐기", "검사 대기", "공정 진행 중", "등록"):
        rows.append(_row("status_distribution", state, count=statuses[state]))

    rows.append(_metrics("inspection_metrics", "ALL", first, all_results,
                         len(rejected_inspections), sum(unperformed.values())))
    for inspection in sorted(set(inspection_ids) | {event.get("inspection") or "" for event in rejected_inspections}):
        rows.append(_metrics(
            "inspection_metrics", inspection,
            [item for item in first if item.inspection == inspection],
            [item for item in all_results if item.inspection == inspection],
            sum((event.get("inspection") or "") == inspection for event in rejected_inspections), unperformed[inspection],

## 역할 교체 (2026-10-30, 5단계부터)
- 구현과 수정은 Claude(Claude Code), 읽기 전용 검수는 Codex(`gpt-6-astra`)가 맡는다. 위 내용과 1–4단계 기록은 당시 역할이다.
- 규칙은 `CLAUDE.md`(구현), `AGENTS.md`(검수), `.claude/settings.json`, `tools/review-loop.ps1`이 기준이다.
- `handoff/gpt-log.md`는 이력을 잇기 위해 이름을 유지하고, 구현 담당 기록으로 계속 쓴다.
- 재가동 효과(3:2, 시드 0–99): 전면 정지 실행은 49회에서 3회로, 미완료 최대는 417개에서 26개로 줄었습니다. 대신 결함품 완료 평균은 0.16에서 0.30으로 늘었습니다. 재가동 없이는 멈춰 있던 제품까지 끝까지 흐르기 때문으로 보이며, 원인은 별도로 검증하지 않았습니다.
- 평가는 규칙을 제안만 합니다. 설비 파일의 `stop_rule`은 바꾸지 않았습니다.

### 이번 단계 제외 범위

실제 시각·UPH·OEE, 공정 설비, 로트 ID, 측정값·Cpk, 그래프, 생산 중간에 시작되는 고장, 재가동 취소는 포함하지 않습니다.

## 격리 범위와 로트 (v2 6단계)

설비가 멈췄을 때 이미 PASS로 지나간 제품 중 어디까지 의심할지 뽑고, 방식별 포착률과 격리량을 비교합니다. 격리는 분석 보고서이며 제품 상태를 바꾸지 않습니다.

**생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다.** 이 단계의 생성 가정은 5단계와 달라 수치를 직접 비교할 수 없습니다.

### 로트 모드

설비 파일에 `"lot_tracking": true`가 있을 때만 켜집니다. CSV는 기존 7열 뒤에 `lot`을 붙인 8열이고, `REGISTER`에서만 씁니다. 형식은 `^[A-Z0-9_-]+$`입니다. products 보고서에는 `model` 뒤에 `lot` 열이 붙습니다.

```powershell
py -m src.main data/sample_events_lot.csv --recipe recipes/lamp_models_retest.json --equipment recipes/equipment_lot.json --summary --containment
```

### 격리 보고서

`--containment`는 설비 모드에서 `reports/<입력>_containment.csv`를 만듭니다. 대상은 정지 시점까지 그 설비에서 최초 검사 PASS를 받은 제품입니다. 재검사 PASS는 제외하고, 다른 검사로 보류·폐기된 제품은 포함합니다. 구간은 그 설비의 허용된 가장 최근 재가동 이후입니다.

| 방식 | 의심 범위 |
| --- | --- |
| `window` | 정지 창 안의 PASS 제품 |
| `last_10`, `last_20` | 구간의 최근 10·20개 최초 검사 중 PASS 제품 |
| `lot` | 구간의 PASS 제품 중 정지 창 FAIL 제품과 같은 로트 |
| `since_restart` | 구간의 PASS 제품 전체 |

### 평가

`generator_lot.json`은 결함을 제품별 독립으로, `generator_lot_clustered.json`은 로트에 몰리게 만듭니다. 두 설정 모두 기대 결함률 5%, 고장 FAIL 0.10, 추가 검출 손실 0.5(결함품 FAIL 0.45)입니다. 유출은 결함품이 수리 전 고장 설비에서 최초 검사 PASS를 받은 경우입니다.

```powershell
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_dev
py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 1000-1099 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_holdout --holdout --selected-from reports/eval_lot_dev_containment.csv
```

선택 기준은 결과를 보기 전에 고정했습니다(`handoff/decisions.md`, 2026-10-31): 유출품 30건 이상, 유출 실행 20회 이상이면 포착률 0.90 이상 방식 중 평균 격리량이 가장 작은 방식. 지표는 실행별 평균이 아니라 전체 합산입니다.

### 평가 결과

정지 규칙 3:2, 제품 500개, 개발 시드 0–99입니다.

| 방식 | 독립 포착률 | 독립 격리량 | 집중 포착률 | 집중 격리량 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `window` | 0.0410 | 5.65 | 0.0204 | 7.30 |
| `lot` | 0.0738 | 22.85 | 0.0816 | 21.63 |
| `last_10` | 0.1475 | 41.06 | 0.1156 | 51.88 |
| `last_20` | 0.2787 | 84.96 | 0.2585 | 106.31 |
| `since_restart` | 0.8525 | 294.69 | 0.8231 | 323.01 |

- 표본 조건은 충족했습니다(독립 유출 122건·61회, 집중 147건·55회).
- **두 설정 모두 `기준 충족 방식 없음`입니다.** 사전 기준대로 어떤 방식도 고르지 않았고, 기준을 완화하지 않았습니다. 홀드아웃은 `검증할 선택 없음`입니다.
- 포착률이 가장 높은 `since_restart`도 500개 중 평균 295개 이상을 격리해 실용적이지 않습니다. 격리 정밀도는 모든 방식에서 0.01 미만입니다.
- `since_restart`의 미포착은 소수 실행에 몰립니다. 독립 18건은 4회, 집중 26건은 5회 실행에서 나왔습니다. 원인은 검증하지 않았습니다.
- 홀드아웃 시드의 `since_restart` 포착률은 0.9845, 0.9910이지만, 개발 시드 판정을 바꾸는 근거로 쓰지 않습니다.
- 집중 설정에서도 `lot` 방식의 포착률은 낮습니다. 격리 기준이 정지를 일으킨 FAIL 제품의 로트라서, 그 제품들이 고장 설비의 오판정이면 결함 로트와 무관하기 때문으로 추정합니다. 이 원인은 검증하지 않았습니다.

### 이번 단계 제외 범위

측정값, 관리도, 실제 시각, 생산 중간 고장, 자동 격리는 포함하지 않습니다.

"""여러 시드로 설비 정지 규칙을 비교한다. 숨은 진실은 평가에서만 쓰고 판정기에는 주지 않는다."""

import argparse
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import median

from .containment import METHODS, run_scopes
from .generate import build_rows, generator_version, load_setup
from .summary import ASSUMPTION_NOTICE, build_analysis


DEFAULT_CONFIG = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "recipes/generator_restart.json"
MIN_DETECTION = Fraction(95, 100)
MAX_MEAN_DELAY = 10
NO_RULE = "기준 충족 규칙 없음"
MIN_CAPTURE = Fraction(90, 100)
MIN_LEAKS = 30
MIN_LEAK_RUNS = 20
NO_METHOD = "기준 충족 방식 없음"
LOW_SAMPLE = "평가 표본 부족"
NO_SELECTION = "검증할 선택 없음"
CONTAINMENT_COLUMNS = (
    "section", "method", "runs", "leak_runs", "leaks", "stops", "capture_rate", "mean_quarantine",
    "precision", "mean_residual", "value",
)
RUN_COLUMNS = (
    "rule", "seed", "stops", "true_stops", "false_stops", "detected", "delay_sum", "full_outage",
    "unfinished", "held", "normal_held", "defective_completed",
)
RULE_COLUMNS = (
    "section", "rule", "runs", "detection_rate", "mean_delay", "stop_precision", "mean_false_stops",
    "false_stop_runs", "full_outage_runs", "unfinished_min", "unfinished_median", "unfinished_max",
    "mean_held", "mean_normal_held", "mean_defective_completed", "criteria", "value",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunMetrics:
    true_stops: int
    false_stops: int
    detected: int
    delay_sum: int
    full_outage: bool
    unfinished: int
    held: int
    normal_held: int
    defective_completed: int


def run_metrics(analysis, faulty, defective):
    """정지 시점에 수리되지 않은 고장 설비의 정지만 정탐이다."""
    fixed_at = {}
    for restart in analysis.restarts:
        if restart.result == "FIXED":
            fixed_at.setdefault(restart.equipment, restart.row_index)
    first_true = {}
    true_stops = 0
    for stop in analysis.stop_history:
        if stop.equipment in faulty and stop.row_index < fixed_at.get(stop.equipment, stop.row_index + 1):
            true_stops += 1
            first_true.setdefault(stop.equipment, stop)
    delays = [sum(item.equipment == name and item.row_index <= stop.row_index
                  for item in analysis.first_inspections) for name, stop in first_true.items()]

    book = analysis.process.equipment_book.equipment
    timeline = sorted([(stop.row_index, 1, stop.equipment) for stop in analysis.stop_history]
                      + [(item.row_index, 0, item.equipment) for item in analysis.restarts])
    stopped, full_outage = set(), False
    for _, stopping, equipment in timeline:
        if stopping:
            stopped.add(equipment)
        else:
            stopped.discard(equipment)
        full_outage = full_outage or all(name in stopped for name, kind in book.items() if kind == book[equipment])

    products = analysis.process.products
    complete = "레시피 모의 흐름 완료"
    return RunMetrics(
        true_stops, len(analysis.stop_history) - true_stops, len(first_true), sum(delays), full_outage,
        sum(product.status != complete for product in products.values()),
        sum(product.status == "보류" for product in products.values()),
        sum(product.status == "보류" and product_id not in defective for product_id, product in products.items()),
        sum(product.status == complete and product_id in defective for product_id, product in products.items()),
    )


def aggregate(runs, faulty_count):
    count = len(runs)
    stops = sum(run.true_stops + run.false_stops for run in runs)
    detected = sum(run.detected for run in runs)
    unfinished = [run.unfinished for run in runs]
    return {
        "runs": count,
        # 명세대로 분모는 실행 수다. 고장 설비가 여러 대인 단위는 미정이라 evaluate에서 막는다.
        "detection_rate": Fraction(sum(run.detected > 0 for run in runs), count) if faulty_count else None,
        "mean_delay": Fraction(sum(run.delay_sum for run in runs), detected) if detected else None,
        "stop_precision": Fraction(sum(run.true_stops for run in runs), stops) if stops else None,
        "mean_false_stops": Fraction(sum(run.false_stops for run in runs), count),
        "false_stop_runs": sum(run.false_stops > 0 for run in runs),
        "full_outage_runs": sum(run.full_outage for run in runs),
        "unfinished_min": min(unfinished), "unfinished_median": Fraction(median(unfinished)),
        "unfinished_max": max(unfinished),
        "mean_held": Fraction(sum(run.held for run in runs), count),
        "mean_normal_held": Fraction(sum(run.normal_held for run in runs), count),
        "mean_defective_completed": Fraction(sum(run.defective_completed for run in runs), count),
    }


def meets_criteria(metrics):
    return (metrics["detection_rate"] is not None and metrics["mean_delay"] is not None
            and metrics["detection_rate"] >= MIN_DETECTION and metrics["mean_delay"] <= MAX_MEAN_DELAY)


def select_rule(rule_metrics):
    """명세에 미리 고정한 기준이다. 후보가 없으면 완화하지 않고 None을 돌려준다."""
    candidates = [rule for rule, metrics in rule_metrics.items() if meets_criteria(metrics)]
    if not candidates:
        return None
    return min(candidates, key=lambda rule: (
        rule_metrics[rule]["mean_false_stops"], rule_metrics[rule]["mean_delay"],
        rule_metrics[rule]["unfinished_median"], rule,
    ))


def holdout_verdict(metrics):
    return "확인" if meets_criteria(metrics) else "미확인"


def parse_seeds(text):
    start, separator, end = text.partition("-")
    if not separator or not start.isdigit() or not end.isdigit() or int(start) > int(end):
        raise ValueError("--seeds는 시작-끝 형식이고 시작이 끝 이하여야 함: " + text)
    return range(int(start), int(end) + 1)


def parse_rules(text):
    rules = []
    for item in text.split(","):
        window, separator, fails = item.partition(":")
        if not separator or not window.isdigit() or not fails.isdigit():
            raise ValueError("--rules는 window:fails 형식이어야 함: " + item)
        rule = (int(window), int(fails))
        if not 1 <= rule[1] <= rule[0] <= 20:
            raise ValueError("규칙은 1 <= fails <= window <= 20이어야 함: " + item)
        if rule in rules:
            raise ValueError("중복 규칙: " + item)
        rules.append(rule)
    return rules


def evaluate(config_path, seeds, rules, products):
    config, content, recipe, book = load_setup(config_path)
    faulty = set(config["faulty_equipment"])
    if len(faulty) > 1:
        raise ValueError("고장 설비가 여러 대인 검출률 단위는 명세에서 정해지지 않음: " + ", ".join(sorted(faulty)))
    runs, rule_metrics = {}, {}
    for window, fails in rules:
        ruled = replace(book, window=window, fails=fails)
        rule_runs = []
        for seed in seeds:
            generator, _, rows, _ = build_rows(seed, products, config, recipe.models, ruled)
            analysis = build_analysis(rows, recipe.models, ruled)
            rule_runs.append(run_metrics(analysis, faulty, set(generator.defective_products)))
        runs[window, fails] = rule_runs
        rule_metrics[window, fails] = aggregate(rule_runs, len(faulty))
    provenance = {
        "config_sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), "recipe_sha256": recipe.sha256,
        "equipment_sha256": book.sha256,
        "generator_version": generator_version(config),
        "seeds": str(seeds[0]) + "-" + str(seeds[-1]), "products": str(products),
    }
    return runs, rule_metrics, provenance


def _name(rule):
    return str(rule[0]) + ":" + str(rule[1])


def _decimal(value, places):
    if value is None:
        return ""
    exact = Decimal(value.numerator) / Decimal(value.denominator)
    return format(exact.quantize(Decimal(1).scaleb(-places), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP), "." + str(places) + "f")


def rule_rows(rule_metrics, provenance, holdout):
    rows = [dict.fromkeys(RULE_COLUMNS, "") for _ in range(len(provenance) + 1)]
    rows[0].update(section="notice", value=ASSUMPTION_NOTICE)
    for row, (key, value) in zip(rows[1:], provenance.items()):
        row.update(section="provenance", rule=key, value=value)
    for rule, metrics in rule_metrics.items():
        rows.append({
            **dict.fromkeys(RULE_COLUMNS, ""), "section": "rule", "rule": _name(rule), "runs": metrics["runs"],
            "detection_rate": _decimal(metrics["detection_rate"], 4), "mean_delay": _decimal(metrics["mean_delay"], 2),
            "stop_precision": _decimal(metrics["stop_precision"], 4),
            "mean_false_stops": _decimal(metrics["mean_false_stops"], 2),
            "false_stop_runs": metrics["false_stop_runs"], "full_outage_runs": metrics["full_outage_runs"],
            "unfinished_min": metrics["unfinished_min"],
            "unfinished_median": _decimal(metrics["unfinished_median"], 1),
            "unfinished_max": metrics["unfinished_max"],
            "mean_held": _decimal(metrics["mean_held"], 2),
            "mean_normal_held": _decimal(metrics["mean_normal_held"], 2),
            "mean_defective_completed": _decimal(metrics["mean_defective_completed"], 2),
            "criteria": holdout_verdict(metrics),
        })
    if not holdout:
        selected = select_rule(rule_metrics)
        rows.append({**dict.fromkeys(RULE_COLUMNS, ""), "section": "selection",
                     "rule": _name(selected) if selected else "", "value": "선택" if selected else NO_RULE})
    return rows


def run_rows(runs, seeds):
    return [{"rule": _name(rule), "seed": seed, "stops": run.true_stops + run.false_stops,
             "true_stops": run.true_stops, "false_stops": run.false_stops, "detected": run.detected,
             "delay_sum": run.delay_sum, "full_outage": int(run.full_outage), "unfinished": run.unfinished,
             "held": run.held, "normal_held": run.normal_held, "defective_completed": run.defective_completed}
            for rule, rule_runs in runs.items() for seed, run in zip(seeds, rule_runs)]


def write_csv(path, columns, rows):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(target, fieldnames=columns)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def print_rules(rows):
    columns = RULE_COLUMNS[1:-1]
    print(" | ".join(columns))
    for row in rows:
        if row["section"] == "rule":
            print(" | ".join(str(row[column]) for column in columns))
    for row in rows:
        if row["section"] in ("notice", "selection"):
            print((row["rule"] + " " if row["rule"] else "") + row["value"])

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Inspection:
    id: str
    after: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelRecipe:
    steps: Tuple[str, ...]
    inspections: Tuple[Inspection, ...]
    retest_limit: Optional[int] = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecipeBook:
    models: Dict[str, ModelRecipe]
    sha256: str

    @property
    def retest_enabled(self) -> bool:
        return all(model.retest_limit is not None for model in self.models.values())


class RecipeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, errors):
        self.errors = tuple(errors)
        super().__init__("\n".join(self.errors))


def load_recipe(path: Path) -> RecipeBook:
    try:
        content = path.read_bytes()
        data = json.loads(content.decode("utf-8-sig"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError) as error:
        raise RecipeError(["$: 레시피 읽기 또는 JSON 오류: " + str(error)]) from error

    errors = []

    def check_id(value, location):
        if not isinstance(value, str) or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*", value) is None:
            errors.append(location + ": ID 형식은 ^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$ 이어야 함")
            return False
        return True

    def check_keys(value, allowed, location):
        for key in value:
            if key not in allowed:
                errors.append(location + "." + key + ": 허용하지 않은 키")

    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise RecipeError(["$: 최상위는 객체여야 함"])
    if not data:
# Codex 역할: 읽기 전용 검수자

- 너는 Claude(Claude Code)가 작성한 코드와 의견을 검수한다. 코드를 직접 고치지 않는다.
- 처음 시작할 때 `handoff/setup-notes.md`를 읽고 배경을 파악한다.
- 매 검수 시작 시 `handoff/tasks.md`, `handoff/gpt-log.md`, `handoff/decisions.md`, 최근 `git log`와 `git diff`를 읽는다. `handoff/gpt-log.md`는 이름과 달리 5단계부터 구현 담당인 Claude의 기록이다.
- 결과는 `handoff/reviews/NNN-review.md`(번호는 순차 증가)에만 쓴다. 각 항목은 아래 형식을 따른다.
  - 심각도(높음/중간/낮음) / 파일:줄 / 문제 / 근거 / 제안
- 검수 기준은 `handoff/tasks.md` 맨 위 「현행 작업」의 명세와 기대표, 그리고 그 아래 완료 단계들의 보존 조건이다. `docs/`의 기존 문서(재검사 범위)는 이전 기록이므로 기준으로 쓰지 않는다.
- 현행 명세도 Claude가 초안을 썼으므로, 명세 자체의 모순·누락도 지적한다. 다만 기대표는 고치지 않고 "명세 이견"으로 올린다.
- 테스트는 `py -m unittest discover -s tests`로 실행한다(`python`은 이 PC에서 동작하지 않음).
- 테스트를 실행했으면 명령과 결과를 그대로 붙인다. 실행하지 않은 것은 "미검증"으로 표시한다.
- Claude 의견에 동의하는 부분도 근거와 함께 명시한다. 다만 동의는 반례 탐색을 마친 뒤에 한다.
- `src/`, `tests/`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/`는 수정하지 않는다. commit·push·패키지 설치·파일 삭제를 하지 않는다.

## 심각도 기준 (영향으로만 정한다)
- **높음**: 기대표·명세와 다른 판정, 거절 행이 상태를 바꿈, 이력·데이터 손실, 이전 단계 보존 조건 위반, 같은 입력의 결과가 달라짐.
- **중간**: 명세가 직접 다루지 않는 입력에서 잘못되거나 설명할 수 없는 판정, 요구를 실제로는 검증하지 못하는 테스트(빈 검증·항상 통과), 문서·보고서와 실제 동작의 불일치, 공개되지 않은 동작 변경.
- **낮음**: 동작에 영향 없는 문구·구조·가독성.
- 마감·일정·수정 비용은 심각도를 낮추는 근거로 쓰지 않는다. 언제 고칠지는 사용자가 정한다. 제안 칸에 "마감 전에는 수정하지 않는다" 같은 일정 판단을 쓰지 않는다.

## 반례 탐색 (매 검수 필수)
- 명세·기대표에 없는 입력으로 코드를 깨 보는 반례를 **최소 5개** 만든다. 경계값, 규칙 두 개가 겹치는 순서, 거절 직후 입력, 빈 값·중복·대소문자, 이전 단계 모드와의 조합을 우선한다.
- 반례는 `handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_NNN.py`(NNN = 리뷰 번호)에 unittest로 쓰고 `py -m unittest handoff/reviews/probes/test_probe_NNN.py`로 실행한다. 파일 출력이 필요하면 테스트 안에서 `tempfile` 임시 폴더를 쓴다. 저장소의 `reports/`, `data/`를 덮어쓰지 않는다.
- 리뷰 파일에 반례마다 입력, 예상(명세 근거), 실제, 판단을 표로 남긴다. 실패한 반례는 심각도 기준에 따라 지적 사항으로 올린다.

+## 격리 범위와 로트 (v2 6단계)
+
+설비가 멈췄을 때 이미 PASS로 지나간 제품 중 어디까지 의심할지 뽑고, 방식별 포착률과 격리량을 비교합니다. 격리는 분석 보고서이며 제품 상태를 바꾸지 않습니다.
+
+**생성 데이터의 확률은 가정이며 실제 설비 진단 근거가 아니다.** 이 단계의 생성 가정은 5단계와 달라 수치를 직접 비교할 수 없습니다.
+
+### 로트 모드
+
+설비 파일에 `"lot_tracking": true`가 있을 때만 켜집니다. CSV는 기존 7열 뒤에 `lot`을 붙인 8열이고, `REGISTER`에서만 씁니다. 형식은 `^[A-Z0-9_-]+$`입니다. products 보고서에는 `model` 뒤에 `lot` 열이 붙습니다.
+
+```powershell
+py -m src.main data/sample_events_lot.csv --recipe recipes/lamp_models_retest.json --equipment recipes/equipment_lot.json --summary --containment
+```
+
+### 격리 보고서
+
+`--containment`는 설비 모드에서 `reports/<입력>_containment.csv`를 만듭니다. 대상은 정지 시점까지 그 설비에서 최초 검사 PASS를 받은 제품입니다. 재검사 PASS는 제외하고, 다른 검사로 보류·폐기된 제품은 포함합니다. 구간은 그 설비의 허용된 가장 최근 재가동 이후입니다.
+
+| 방식 | 의심 범위 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `window` | 정지 창 안의 PASS 제품 |
+| `last_10`, `last_20` | 구간의 최근 10·20개 최초 검사 중 PASS 제품 |
+| `lot` | 구간의 PASS 제품 중 정지 창 FAIL 제품과 같은 로트 |
+| `since_restart` | 구간의 PASS 제품 전체 |
+
+### 평가
+
+`generator_lot.json`은 결함을 제품별 독립으로, `generator_lot_clustered.json`은 로트에 몰리게 만듭니다. 두 설정 모두 기대 결함률 5%, 고장 FAIL 0.10, 추가 검출 손실 0.5(결함품 FAIL 0.45)입니다. 유출은 결함품이 수리 전 고장 설비에서 최초 검사 PASS를 받은 경우입니다.
+
+```powershell
+py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 0-99 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_dev
+py -m src.evaluate --containment --config recipes/generator_lot.json --seeds 1000-1099 --rules 3:2 --out eval_lot_holdout --holdout --selected-from reports/eval_lot_dev_containment.csv
+```
+
+선택 기준은 결과를 보기 전에 고정했습니다(`handoff/decisions.md`, 2026-10-31): 유출품 30건 이상, 유출 실행 20회 이상이면 포착률 0.90 이상 방식 중 평균 격리량이 가장 작은 방식. 지표는 실행별 평균이 아니라 전체 합산입니다.
+
+### 평가 결과
+
+정지 규칙 3:2, 제품 500개, 개발 시드 0–99입니다.
+
+| 방식 | 독립 포착률 | 독립 격리량 | 집중 포착률 | 집중 격리량 |
+| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
+| `window` | 0.0410 | 5.65 | 0.0204 | 7.30 |
+| `lot` | 0.0738 | 22.85 | 0.0816 | 21.63 |
+| `last_10` | 0.1475 | 41.06 | 0.1156 | 51.88 |
+| `last_20` | 0.2787 | 84.96 | 0.2585 | 106.31 |
+| `since_restart` | 0.8525 | 294.69 | 0.8231 | 323.01 |
+
+- 표본 조건은 충족했습니다(독립 유출 122건·61회, 집중 147건·55회).
+- **두 설정 모두 `기준 충족 방식 없음`입니다.** 사전 기준대로 어떤 방식도 고르지 않았고, 기준을 완화하지 않았습니다. 홀드아웃은 `검증할 선택 없음`입니다.
+- 포착률이 가장 높은 `since_restart`도 500개 중 평균 295개 이상을 격리해 실용적이지 않습니다. 격리 정밀도는 모든 방식에서 0.01 미만입니다.
+- `since_restart`의 미포착은 소수 실행에 몰립니다. 독립 18건은 4회, 집중 26건은 5회 실행에서 나왔습니다. 원인은 검증하지 않았습니다.
+- 홀드아웃 시드의 `since_restart` 포착률은 0.9845, 0.9910이지만, 개발 시드 판정을 바꾸는 근거로 쓰지 않습니다.
+- 집중 설정에서도 `lot` 방식의 포착률은 낮습니다. 격리 기준이 정지를 일으킨 FAIL 제품의 로트라서, 그 제품들이 고장 설비의 오판정이면 결함 로트와 무관하기 때문으로 추정합니다. 이 원인은 검증하지 않았습니다.
+
+### 이번 단계 제외 범위
+
+측정값, 관리도, 실제 시각, 생산 중간 고장, 자동 격리는 포함하지 않습니다.

import hashlib

import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from src.generate import GENERATOR_VERSION, Generator, generate
from src.equipment import load_equipment

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

class GeneratorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_t51_defect_probability_applies_once_to_initial_and_retest_results(self):
        models = load_recipe(ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json").models
        book = load_equipment(ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json", models)
        config = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        cases = (
            ([0.019, 0.899, 0.69, 0.899, 0.69, 0.899], ["FAIL"] * 3, ["p"]),
            ([0.019, 0.9, 0.9, 0.9], ["PASS"] * 3, ["p"]),
            ([0.02, 0.02, 0.02, 0.02], ["PASS"] * 3, []),
        )
        for draws, expected, defective in cases:
            with self.subTest(draws=draws):
                generator = Generator(42, config, models, book)
                with patch.object(generator.rng, "random", side_effect=draws):
                    rows = [row for row, _ in generator.product("p", "A")]
                results = [row for row in rows if row["event"] == "INSPECT"]
                self.assertEqual([row["result"] for row in results], expected)
                self.assertEqual(generator.defective_products, defective)
                self.assertTrue(all(tuple(row) == EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS for row in rows))

    def test_t51_seed42_meta_lists_defects_without_csv_truth_columns(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            target = Path(directory) / "events.csv"
            meta = generate(42, 500, target, CONFIG)
            defective = meta["defective_products"]
            self.assertEqual(meta["defective_count"], len(defective))
            self.assertEqual(len(defective), len(set(defective)))
            self.assertGreater(len(defective), 0)
            events = read_events(target, EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS)
            registered = {row["product_id"] for row in events if row["event"] == "REGISTER"}
            self.assertTrue(set(defective) <= registered)
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(Path(str(target) + ".meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")), meta)

    def test_t42_same_seed_csv_meta_and_provenance(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            a, b = Path(directory) / "a.csv", Path(directory) / "b.csv"
            first = generate(42, 500, a, CONFIG)
            generate(42, 500, b, CONFIG)
            self.assertEqual(a.read_bytes(), b.read_bytes())
            self.assertEqual(Path(str(a) + ".meta.json").read_bytes(),
                             Path(str(b) + ".meta.json").read_bytes())
            self.assertEqual((first["seed"], first["products"], first["generator_version"]),
                             (42, 500, GENERATOR_VERSION))
            for key, path in (("config", CONFIG), ("recipe", ROOT / "recipes/lamp_models_retest.json"),
                              ("equipment", ROOT / "recipes/equipment_generated.json")):
                self.assertEqual(first["sha256"][key], hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest())
            self.assertEqual(first["rows"], first["normal_rows"] + sum(first["injected_errors"].values()))
            counts = list(first["injected_errors"].values())
            self.assertLessEqual(max(counts) - min(counts), 1)
            self.assertEqual(sum(counts), int(first["normal_rows"] * 0.03))

    def test_t43_different_seed_changes_csv(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            a, b = Path(directory) / "a.csv", Path(directory) / "b.csv"
            generate(42, 500, a, CONFIG)
            generate(43, 500, b, CONFIG)
            self.assertNotEqual(a.read_bytes(), b.read_bytes())

    def test_review005_m3_missing_candidate_explains_failure_without_output(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            target = Path(directory) / "small.csv"
            result = subprocess.run(
                ["py", "-m", "src.generate", "--seed", "39", "--products", "4", "--out", str(target),
                 "--config", str(CONFIG)], cwd=directory, capture_output=True,
                env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT), "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stderr.decode("utf-8"))

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RECIPE = ROOT / "recipes" / "lamp_models_retest.json"
RESTART_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment_restart.json"
PLAIN_EQUIPMENT = ROOT / "recipes" / "equipment_generated.json"
RESTART_CONFIG = ROOT / "recipes" / "generator_restart.json"


def row(seq, product_id, name, inspection="", result="", model="", equipment=""):
    return dict(zip(EQUIPMENT_INPUT_COLUMNS, (str(seq), product_id, name, inspection, result, model, equipment)))


def prepared(products):
    rows = []
    for product_id in products:
        for name, model in (("REGISTER", "B"), ("P1", ""), ("P2", "")):
            rows.append(row(len(rows) + 1, product_id, name, model=model))
    return rows


def leak(seq, product_id, result, equipment="LEAK-1"):
    return row(seq, product_id, "INSPECT", "LEAK", result, equipment=equipment)


def restart(seq, result="NO_FAULT", equipment="LEAK-1", product_id="", inspection="", model=""):
    return row(seq, product_id, "EQUIP_RESTART", inspection, result, model, equipment)

        decisions = []
        for item in rows:
# Determining the reviewed diff and scope

Read this at Stage 1. It defines how to resolve scope on every invocation path and how to compute the deterministic scope signals that Stage 3 (Select reviewers) uses.

### Stage 1: Determine scope

Compute the diff range, file list, and diff. Minimize permission prompts by combining into as few commands as possible.

**If `base:` argument is provided (fast path):**

The caller already knows the diff base. Skip all base-branch detection, remote resolution, and merge-base computation. Use the provided value directly:

```
BASE_ARG="{base_arg}"
BASE=$(git merge-base HEAD "$BASE_ARG" 2>/dev/null) || BASE="$BASE_ARG"
```

Then produce the same output as the other paths:

```
echo "BASE:$BASE" && echo "FILES:" && git diff --name-only $BASE && echo "DIFF:" && git diff -U10 --irreversible-delete $BASE && echo "UNTRACKED:" && git ls-files --others --exclude-standard
```

This path works with any ref — a SHA, `origin/main`, a branch name. Callers reviewing the current checkout should pass explicit `base:` when auto-detection is unnecessary. **Do not combine `base:` with a PR number or branch target.** If both are present, stop with an error: "Cannot use `base:` with a PR number or branch target — `base:` implies the current checkout is already the correct branch. Pass `base:` alone, or pass the target alone and let scope detection resolve the base."

**If a PR number or GitHub URL is provided as an argument:**

Do **not** check out the PR branch. Scope comes from GitHub read APIs plus optional local alignment when HEAD already matches the PR head branch.

**Skip-condition pre-check.** Before scope detection, run a PR-state probe:

```
gh pr view <number-or-url> --json state,title,body,files
```

Apply skip rules in order:

- `state` is `CLOSED` or `MERGED` -> stop with reason `PR is closed/merged; not reviewing.`
- **Trivial-PR judgment**: spawn a lightweight sub-agent on the platform's cheapest capable model when a known override exists; otherwise omit the model override and inherit. Give it the PR title, body, and changed file paths. The agent's task: "Is this an automated or trivial PR that does not warrant a code review? Consider: dependency lock-file or manifest-only bumps, automated release commits, chore version increments with no substantive code changes. When in doubt, answer no — false negatives (skipped reviews that should have run) are more costly than false positives (unnecessary reviews)." If the judgment returns yes: stop with reason `PR appears to be a trivial automated PR; not reviewing. Run without a PR argument to review the current branch, or pass base:<ref> if review is intended.`

If the user's or project's active instructions or the live prompt name a model for this subagent, use that model instead of the default tier above. They can name it by the subagent's tier, by its prompt-asset name, or for the subagents being dispatched. Without such an instruction, a model configured for this subagent's role takes precedence over the default tier.

When any skip rule applies, stop without dispatching reviewers. **Default mode:** emit the reason as plain text. **`mode:agent`:** emit JSON only — `{"status":"skipped","reason":"<same message>"}` — so programmatic callers can parse the outcome. **Standalone**, **`base:`**, and **branch-remote** paths are unaffected. **Draft PRs are reviewed normally.**

If no skip rule applies, fetch PR metadata **without checkout**:

```
gh pr view <number-or-url> --json title,body,baseRefName,headRefName,headRefOid,isCrossRepository,url,files,reviews,comments --jq '{title, body, baseRefName, headRefName, headRefOid, isCrossRepository, url, files: [.files[].path], hasPriorComments: ((.reviews | map(select(.state != "APPROVED" or .body != "")) | length) > 0 or (.comments | length) > 0)}'
```

Set `BASE:` to `pr:<number-or-url>` (logical marker — not a git SHA). Set `UNTRACKED:` from `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` on the **current** checkout (usually empty during PR-remote review).

**PR scope mode.** Classify as **`local-aligned`** only when **all** of these hold; otherwise use **`pr-remote`**. A matching branch name alone is not enough — a fork PR or a stale local branch can share a name with the PR head while pointing at unrelated code, and trusting the name would diff and inspect the wrong tree.

1. `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` equals `headRefName`.
2. The PR is **not** cross-repository (`isCrossRepository` is false).
3. The PR head commit is contained in the local checkout: `git merge-base --is-ancestor <headRefOid> HEAD` exits 0. This confirms the working tree actually carries the PR head (allowing unpushed local fixes layered on top) rather than an unrelated same-named branch.

- **`local-aligned`** — all three checks pass. Local Read/Grep/git blame against workspace files are valid for PR changed paths.
- **`pr-remote`** — any check fails. The working tree is **not** the PR head; workspace file contents for changed paths may be stale or unrelated.

**Diff by scope mode** (do not mix remote and local diffs — contradictory hunks cause false positives):

- **`local-aligned`:** Resolve `<resolved-base-ref>` from `baseRefName` (fetch if needed). Compute `BASE=$(git merge-base HEAD <resolved-base-ref>)`, then set `FILES:` from `git diff --name-only $BASE` and `DIFF:` from `git diff -U10 --irreversible-delete $BASE` (includes committed, staged, and unstaged changes on the PR branch). Do **not** call `gh pr diff` or append remote hunks — when unpushed fixes exist, the local tree is canonical. Note in Coverage: `scope: local-aligned (PR; local tree diff)`.
- **`pr-remote`:** Set `FILES:` from the PR `files` array. Set `DIFF:` from `gh pr diff <number-or-url> --color=never`. If `gh pr diff` fails, stop with an actionable error — do not fall back to checkout. Then drop deleted-file bodies from that saved output the way `--irreversible-delete` does on the local paths, with `awk '/^diff --git /{del=0} del && !/^index /{next} /^deleted file mode /{del=1} {print}'`; never pipe `gh pr diff` straight into the filter, because the pipe hides its failure.

When **`pr-remote`**, before Stage 4:

1. Best-effort fetch PR head without checkout: `git fetch --no-tags origin <headRefName>:refs/review/pr-<number>-head` (substitute PR number from metadata).
2. Set `PR_HEAD_REF=refs/review/pr-<number>-head` for reviewers and validators only when `git rev-parse refs/review/pr-<number>-head` equals the metadata `headRefOid`. A successful fetch does not prove the ref is the PR head: on a cross-repository PR, `origin` can carry an unrelated branch with the same name, and the PR can move between the metadata call and the fetch. When the fetch fails or the OID differs, omit `PR_HEAD_REF` and note which in Coverage — reviewers must rely on diff hunks only.
3. Best-effort fetch the PR base without checkout: `git fetch --no-tags origin <baseRefName>`. When it succeeds, resolve a concrete ref with `git rev-parse FETCH_HEAD` and set `PR_BASE_REF` to that SHA — a **real git base ref** reviewers and validators use for file-level git diffs (e.g. `data-migration-reviewer` runs `git diff <PR_BASE_REF> -- db/schema.rb`/`structure.sql`). The `pr:<number-or-url>` logical marker in `BASE:` stays the scope marker; `PR_BASE_REF` is the diffable base. When the fetch fails, omit `PR_BASE_REF` and note in Coverage — schema-drift and other git-diff checks fall back to diff hunks only and must **not** assume `main`.
4. Include `<pr-scope-mode>pr-remote</pr-scope-mode>` and, when set, `<pr-head-ref>...</pr-head-ref>` and `<pr-base-ref>...</pr-base-ref>` in the Stage 4 review context bundle.

Reviewers and Stage 5b validators in **`pr-remote`** mode must **not** Read/Grep workspace paths for files in `FILES:`. Inspect via `git show <PR_HEAD_REF>:<path>` when `PR_HEAD_REF` is set, otherwise use only the provided diff hunks. **`local-aligned`** uses normal workspace inspection.

**If a branch name is provided as an argument:**

Substitute the provided branch name as `<branch>`. Do **not** check out `<branch>`.

If `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` equals `<branch>`, use the **standalone (current branch)** path below — same tree, explicit branch name; do not use remote-only diff.

Otherwise diff the remote/local ref **without checkout**:

1. Try `gh pr view <branch> --json baseRefName,url,headRefName` — if a PR exists, prefer the **PR number/URL path** above (same remote diff rules).
2. Else resolve `<branch>` as `origin/<branch>` or `<branch>` after `git fetch --no-tags origin <branch>` when needed.
